117. E1 — exact source claims and their quantifier scope

  1. p2a:thm:v3101-generated-coconsumption, lines 9605–9638. On one occurring root and a result-independent licensed constructor whose predeclared required output is the complete typed tuple Q=(q_1,...,q_N), GP2 retains all required nonredundant constituents in their native sorts. A later constructor taking that full typed Q cannot delete or rebind constituents while claiming to use the same output. The proof cites p2:thm:generated-constituent-corealization (2045–2075), p2:cor:no-idle-licensed-constructor (3049–3077), and typed role restrictions. Status: VERIFIED WITH EXPLICIT CONSTRUCTOR-ORIGIN GUARD. Its final sentences exclude the different, false proposition that every constituent is essential to every subsequent numerical readout.
  2. p2a:prop:v281-complete-input-factorization, lines 9668–9711. For a surviving complete output family Q and licensed readout F, factorization through coordinate restriction π_S exists and is unique on π_S(Q) iff F is constant on every π_S fibre. The proof uses only well-definedness and surjectivity onto the image. It additionally says numerical factorization does not license a new physical deletion/compression constructor. Status: VERIFIED, MATHEMATICAL VALUE-FACTOR TEST AND PHYSICAL-GRAMMAR FIREWALL.
  3. p2a:thm:v3102-preterminal-relation-preservation, lines 10721–10753. Assumes one occurring parent generates typed channels T_a and embeddings E_a into a finite receiver (Q,K_Q), and that a licensed action constructor consumes B=Σ_a E_aT_a. Where the licensed quadratic return uses the defined inverse K_Q^{-1}, distributivity gives B†K_Q^{-1}B=Σ_{a,b}T_a† E_a† K_Q^{-1}E_b T_b. The upstream co-consumption theorem preserves the nonredundant mixed relations in that frozen action word. A later noninjective readout q does not license a replacement using only (q(E_aT_a))_a absent a theorem proving relation faithfulness for the required cross returns. Status: VERIFIED within the stated complete frozen quadratic-action and inverse-defined domain. It is not an unconditional claim that every generated pair produces a physically realized nonzero cross term.
  4. p2a:cor:v3102-construct-before-terminalize, lines 10755–10768. For a physical claim whose declared owner incidence is actually carried by cross-channel returns, the licensed chronology is generated parent channels → complete action contraction → owner/family return → terminal scalar/support readout. Reassembling lost incidence from separate scalars is a different constructor without an independently generated relation-faithful lift. Status: VERIFIED CONDITIONALLY. It does not prohibit early scalarization when the claim requires only a terminal value and factorization/relation faithfulness is licensed.
  5. p2a:cor:v3103-sibling-output-consumption, lines 10785–10811. If the predeclared complete output includes sibling pairs (m_r,ρ_r) and a later frozen licensed constructor takes their joint typed tuple, the scalar can be a later coefficient without selecting the relation. Discarding ρ_r and reconstructing it from m_r requires a separate relation-faithfulness certificate. Its proof consumes both the v3.101 and v3.102 results. Status: VERIFIED CONDITIONALLY; NO PHYSICAL SOURCE SORT OR NEW COEFFICIENT CREATED.

119. E3 — precise mathematical analysis, failure cases and sharpness

Finite noncommuting rectangular expansion. If the typed maps E_aT_a:H→Q share a common domain and K_Q:Q→Q is invertible, multiplication and adjunction give

(Σ_a E_aT_a)† K_Q^{-1}(Σ_b E_bT_b) = Σ_{a,b}T_a† E_a† K_Q^{-1}E_b T_b.

For Hermitian positive K_Q, the full return is Hermitian nonnegative. The ordered (a,b) and (b,a) cross terms are adjoints; neither can generally be deleted separately. In the source, B†K_Q^{-1}B denotes the quadratic return. The overall sign and numerical prefactor of a fully integrated on-shell action depend on its prior declared action convention; they are not determined from the presence of B alone. The theorem is valid as a retained named quadratic word, not as a universal formula for arbitrary nonlinear receiver elimination.

Countermodel to premature norm readout. Let Q=C, K_Q=1, E_1=E_2=1, q(z)=|z|. The two tuples (T_1,T_2)=(1,1) and (1,-1) have equal separate terminal data (1,1), while the complete returns are |1+1|²=4 and |1-1|²=0. Hence no function on the scalar pair (q(T_1),q(T_2)) can reproduce this return on a family containing both tuples. This is a mathematical stress test; it is not asserted that both tuples are complete physical GP2-admissible candidates in a particular Born–Hopf action.

Sharp nonimplication. Noninjectivity of q by itself does not force cross-return loss on every claim. If Q=C², K_Q=I and E_1,E_2 are orthogonal coordinate embeddings, then E_1†K_Q^{-1}E_2=0 and F=|T_1|²+|T_2|² factors through the separate norms. Alternatively, if a frozen generated family requires T_2=T_1, the complete F=4|T_1|² also factors through the norms on that family. These are scoped mathematical sufficiency examples, not retroactive licenses for a new physical scalar-only constructor.

Sharp criterion. Let X be the actual admissible generated tuple family, q^×:X→Y the separate terminal map, and F:X→Z the full required value. A unique map \bar F:q^×(X)→Z with F=\bar F∘q^× exists iff q^×(x)=q^×(x') ⇒ F(x)=F(x') for all x,x'∈X. For typed cross relations themselves to be retained, the applicable source theorem requires their own relation-faithful descent, not merely a coincidentally constant total scalar. The full complex Gram matrix can retain coherent cross terms modulo a common phase while still being a noninjective output; this illustrates an allowed independently certified sufficient quotient, not an added GP.

Domain edge. If K_Q is singular, K_Q^{-1} is undefined as displayed. A Moore–Penrose inverse is another licensed word with kernel and range compatibility obligations; it is not identical to a true inverse. Thus the source's inverse expression is read in its defined inverse domain. The standard scalar quadratic action 1/2 y²-by has on-shell value -b²/2; this also illustrates why one must not automatically equate the nonnegative quadratic return to an on-shell action without the established sign/factor convention.

126. E3 — Independent mathematical replay

Let N=|X|≥1 and identify the label carrier as C^(N²) with orthonormal e_(x,y). Put e=e_(x0,x0). Any linear A satisfying Ae=0 and Ae_v=e_v for every other basis vector v equals Q=I-ee* on an exhaustive basis. Uniqueness does not require self-adjointness or an additional GP3 optimization. The resulting Q is self-adjoint/idempotent of rank N²−1. The specified Ω=(1/N)Σ_v e_v has norm one, so ⟨Ω,QΩ⟩=1−|⟨e,Ω⟩|²=1−1/N². For N=5, the value is 24/25. The nonuniform rooted vector e itself has expectation zero; the invariant-vector expectation is not universal over arbitrary preparations. The uniform vector is permutation-invariant under the induced label permutation, but the rooted projector is not full-group-invariant unless the root is fixed: it is equivariant only for the root stabilizer in general.

For group I, subgroup H with index five and coset representatives g_0,...,g_4, the 25 labelled translates g_i H g_j^{-1} count every group element five times: fixing j, the five left cosets g_i H g_j^{-1} partition the group. Removing g_0H g_0^{-1}=H gives

(1/(25|H|)) Σ_(i,j)≠(0,0) Σ_(q∈g_i H g_j^-1) F(q) = (5/(25|H|)) Σ_(q∈I)F(q) − (1/(25|H|)) Σ_(q∈H)F(q) = τ_I(F) − (1/25)τ_H(F);

for |H|=24, |I|=120, the denominator is 600 and total retained weight is 24/25. The 25 cell registers overlap as point sets, so the identity requires labelled evaluation registers, not disjoint subsets of I. Tensoring label deletion with the already retained C^24 payload gives rank(Q⊗I_24)=576 inside a 600-dimensional labelled carrier; it does not identify that carrier with C^120 vertices.

The I-B nested Reynolds formula P_I−P_H/25 uses H⊂I and orthogonal inclusions ran P_I⊂ran P_H. The three eigenvalues follow on the decomposition ran P_I⊕(ran P_H⊖ran P_I)⊕ker P_H: 24/25, −1/25, 0. This linear-algebra identity is proved; degree-specific ranks at 6 and 12 are imported from the named I-C character calculation, not independently recomputed here.

129. Exact statement, sorts, quantifiers and the physical claim

  • Input: an occurring licensed constructor has already produced native terminal coefficient m_r of scalar or role-tagged-coefficient sort, possibly together with retained sibling incidence rho_r. This is an antecedent, not a fresh proof that a specific mass value or a physical pole exists.
  • Later construction: A=A(m_r,rho_r,...) is formed by a subsequently licensed finite operator constructor, with chronological order frozen before evaluating A.
  • Forbidden unlicensed inference: from the existence of A and a mathematical spectral functional s(A), do not replace the earlier native generating rule by m_r := s(A), or identify the two as a physical equality, without a separately licensed relation theorem. This applies to eigenvalues, singular values, Takagi values and other spectral functionals.
  • Allowed separate conclusion: if a legitimate, already licensed theorem proves m_r=s(A) on a specified domain, this is a derived downstream comparison dictionary. It does not change which constructor generated m_r. Physical ownership of source coefficients remains at the earlier terminal operation.
  • Corollary: Yukawa/CKM/PMNS/mass-matrix/Takagi coordinates can encode previously generated packets. They are not thereby native terminal-mass generators; the correct native flavor claims use the actually generated current/incidence and propagation maps.

Equal physical value by later spectral relation II-A 10927–10930 SPECIALIZED EXCEPTION GUARD: separately licensed relation theorem Needed only for the positive claim m_r=s(A) as a physical identification; not a new fundamental principle.

Formal deduction. Let w_m be the frozen physical constructor word generating typed output m_r, and let w_A be a later licensed word with an input edge from m_r. These directed provenance edges cannot be reversed without replacing the fixed grammar. An algebraic spectral map s applied to A may yield a value equal to m_r, but equality of values does not prove equality of generating rules or physical ownership. Postulating a new cross-sort equality without a generated relation would violate the GP2 no-insertion and typed-output clauses; proving it from a licensed relation yields a legitimate derived comparison, not retroactive mass generation. That is exactly the source claim.

Potentially misleading step that does NOT create a gap. The printed proof (10934–10937) mentions loss at a noninjective readout; the theorem does not assume the spectral map is always noninjective. Noninjectivity is a possible mathematical obstruction, not an indispensable premise of the chronology firewall. Even when s(A) is injective on a declared subfamily and m_r is algebraically recoverable, its later recovery does not alter the earlier generated provenance. GP2/frozen chronology independently establish the actual theorem.

131. E3 sharp mathematical distinction

Noninjective spectral value. A(m)=diag(m,-m). For m=1 or m=-1, the singular-value multiset is {1,1}. Therefore no universal function of the singular values alone returns the earlier signed m on a family containing both configurations. Similarly, the 1×1 complex symmetric Takagi value is |m|, which loses phase information.

Valid comparison equality, not an inverse constructor. On the already declared m>0 domain, A(m)=mI_2 has both eigenvalues and both singular values equal to m. If the algebraic relation is licensed for the physical target, the equality is a valid downstream dictionary. It does not transform the spectral operation into the original mass-generation word.

Flavor/current nonimplication. Let H_u=diag(1,2) and H_d=diag(3,4). For R a nontrivial real orthogonal rotation, H_d'=R H_d R^T has exactly the same eigenvalues as H_d, while its relative eigenframe/current-coordinate matrix changes. The terminal spectra alone do not determine relative weak-current incidence; a separately generated current morphism must be consumed by a native flavor observable claim.

Source packet compression. With two terminal role coefficients (m_1,m_2), the later 1×1 incidence sum R=m_1+m_2 takes the same value on (1,2) and (2,1). This shows why role-tagged provenance cannot in general be replaced by a scalar after the fact. The example is a mathematical control, not a demonstration that these tuples are both physically GP2-admissible in the framework.

Batch 23 — Physical-descendant exclusivity and retained bulk scalaron

Authoritative source: Paper2A-GeometricConstitution-v3_206.tex (SHA-256 167aec2ef3b1c3f30859ca65cf430778440887466a4451d47e6702af0edb9be0), framework v2.358. Coverage: Corollary p2a:cor:v3106-physical-descendant-exclusivity (10962–10993), cosmological remark p2a:rem:v3106-k16-use (10995–11003), its one-way successor qualification (11041–11049), named upstream rules and limited VI-A downstream source check. No original paper or global core graph edited.

135. Precise theorem, domain and intended observable

One physical action S_* exists by hypothesis. A licensed boundary/interior constructor admits stationary elimination q_I=bar q_I(q_partial;y) with S_*^partial(q_partial;y)=S_*(q_partial,bar q_I(q_partial;y);y). The source also assumes a downstream theorem has independently certified S_*^partial as the physical descendant consumed in a declared target role. The frozen physical grammar contains no second owner, carrier or role reconstituting the eliminated interior as an autonomous downstream object. Under exactly those conditions, an independently evolved copy of the eliminated interior cannot be treated as a coequal downstream physical descendant of the same occurrence. The original parent remains as derivational data and can remain physical on its independently established bulk-history role.

The targeted physical distinction is absence of duplicated downstream degrees of freedom, not a theorem about eliminating all solutions of the parent dynamics, selecting an ordinary-particle owner from Schur reduction alone or altering K16 Cauchy data.

Citation-domain challenge adjudicated: the finite-object lemma does not, on its own, apply wholesale to dynamical action variables. The corollary nevertheless already assumes the licensed descendant and no second generated independent role. An extra coequal autonomous child would add an ungenerated role to the same frozen physical presentation, contradicting GP2 no insertion. This establishes the negative conclusion at the claim's precise scope without silently extending the specialized lemma. Generated co-consumption ensures the previously licensed reduced relation is not replaced or renormalized. No new necessary proof gap certified.

137. E3 — Exact stationary reduction and one-way Cauchy distinction

For a finite quadratic action S(x,z)=a x²/2 + b xz + c z²/2, with c≠0 and interior stationary equation b x+c z=0, one has

bar z(x)=-(b/c)x, S^partial(x)=1/2(a-b²/c)x², H_eff=a-b²/c.

This is the original action evaluated on its stationary interior graph and contains the interior response. Adding a new, independently normalized quadratic child would generally change that response and is not an identity derived from the same action. With singular interior Hessian one must retain the constraint relation rather than assert an unconstrained Schur graph.

Crucially, a stationary replacement is not an exact replacement for all dynamical histories. For z¨+z=0 with z(0)=0, z˙(0)=1, the solution sin t continues despite a zero stationary-source solution. The K16 source, Paper VI-A 9112–9132 and 9434–9497, makes the same type distinction: the Schur response uses the same homogeneous scalaron, while its bulk evolution retains inherited Cauchy data. This mathematical analogy is not an independent audit of the K16 dynamical construction.

Proofs/Paper2A-PhysicalDescendantBatch23-v1_1-verify.py and its result JSON run 20/20 PASS (exact SymPy), checking scalar/vector stationary Schur algebra, source-conditioned reduction, independently added quadratic-child mismatch, singular stationary constraints and non-reset bulk oscillator dynamics. The tests certify mathematical sharpness, not physical actualization or GP3 selection.

Batch 24 — Boundary response owner, full stress and fixed-owner cells

Standalone detailed source/proof report: Audits/Paper2A-BoundaryResponseOwnerBatch24-v1_1.md. The following incorporates its mathematical audit, cross-paper scope boundary and test evidence.

140. Exact target and conclusion

The source presupposes an already occurring licensed same-action construction whose output is a physical boundary descendant S_*^∂ on the declared tangent J_∂. A downstream theorem independently constitutes a particle carrier J_P and supplies an owner-preserving inclusion/restriction ι_P:J_P→J_∂ into the retained response domain. On that antecedent domain, an induced particle response (including licensed Schur, pole or positive-frequency restrictions) is a restriction of the same boundary response. The source explicitly does not infer complete boundary-stress or energy-packet identities:

T_{∂,dyn}^{μν}=T_ord^{μν}, Q_∂=Q_ord.

Such a stronger assertion needs an independently generated common-owner relation or equivalent typed theorem. Crucially, that is a necessary provenance condition, not by itself a sufficient test of equality of full functional stress: the source's downstream VI-A action-jet certificate separately checks the functional identity.

The adjacent v3107 remark is one-way: no reintroduction of an unlicensed coequal interior child after a physical stationary descendant has been separately certified; the existence of that stationary descendant does not select its particle owner.

The adjacent v3108 corollary is also conditional: if a complete response cell uses the same fixed normalized boundary datum q, varying solely through η(ρ); no cell constructor changes q's target sort; and an independent endpoint/common-parent theorem physically types q as owner P, then every nonzero cell member carries that owner. The same generated normalization excludes an extra arbitrary efficiency multiplier unless separately generated.

Core derivation: The constructor is licensed to provide S_*^∂ on J_∂, and the independently typed ι_P gives a legitimate restriction K_P=ι_P^*K_∂ι_P of the response bilinear form on J_P. Promotion of the whole boundary energy or stress to ordinary-particle ownership is a different cross-sort statement. Because this output/identification is neither an already declared consequence of the restriction nor a generated relation in the theorem's premises, GP2 no insertion forbids silently adding it. This establishes the negative implication without denying that an independently generated stronger theorem can later prove the equality.

Specialized no-splitting caution: The original same-role lemma (p2:lem:gp2-same-role-no-splitting, 3196–3235) treats finite internal object images. It should not be applied as a universal theorem deleting all dynamical action modes. Here the source's negative owner-identification claim follows directly from GP2 no-insertion, typed-output scope and the stated independent owner guards.

142. E3/E4 independent mathematical controls

Response restriction does not determine complete stress. Choose J_∂=R², J_P=span(e₁), K_∂=diag(2,5), ι_P(t)=(t,0). Then K_P=ι_P^T K_∂ι_P=2, yet K_∂≠diag(2,0) and the complementary tangent has nonzero response 5. Even two full responses diag(2,5) and diag(2,17) have the same particle restriction. This proves that response support on a physical particle carrier does not, by itself, determine the entire boundary response, let alone its stress tensor.

Hessian equality does not imply stress equality. Let g be a metric variable, η a cell variable, and q a field coordinate. The two finite local actions

S_part(q;g,η)=(g+η)q²/2, S_∂(q;g,η)=S_part(q;g,η)+c(g,η), c(g,η)=g²+3η

have identical q-Hessians and identical first q-derivatives on the entire chart, yet ∂_g(S_∂−S_part)=2g and ∂_η(S_∂−S_part)=3. Any complete stress or work defined by these variations can differ. An added affine term ℓ(g,η)q is also invisible to the q-Hessian but is detected by the first field jet. Conversely, if c is constant in g,η, those additional derivatives vanish. This directly checks VI-A D19's mathematical certificate: same Hessian + first field jet leaves c(g,η), and the stronger metric/cell stress identity requires its variation to vanish.

Moving projectors have mixed jets. If K=diag(2,5) and ι(θ)=(cos θ,sin θ), then K_P(θ)=2cos²θ+5sin²θ and ∂_θK_P=3sin2θ; at θ=π/4 the derivative is 3 despite ∂_θK=0. Correct differentiation requires ∂_θι^* Kι+ι^*K∂_θι in addition to ι^*(∂_θ K)ι. Therefore an inherited response carrier alone does not certify off-shell metric/cell stress equality when the inclusion/projector moves.

Fixed-owner scalar cell. For S_∂^(2)[q;ρ]=η(ρ) S_0(q), an already declared fixed owner of q is carried through every nonzero response in the same constructor. If η=0, there is no nonzero response from which to infer a physical owner. Inserting an independent multiplier ξ changes the frozen scalar response unless a separate deformation or normalization relation has been licensed. This is the exact scope of v3108, not a new physical efficiency prediction.

The companion executable Proofs/Paper2A-BoundaryOwnerBatch24-v1_1-verify.py performed 29/29 passing exact SymPy controls. These are counter-implication tests and mathematical replay, not certificates that a particular physical particle stress tensor is realized.

143. Direct K16 consumer and limited certificate

The current VI-A source's later authoritative owner theorem p8:thm:v2136-k16-direct-particle-owner (9434–9497) claims the direct K8→K16 packet's boundary owner is the constituted particle-boundary block; it explicitly preserves the homogeneous scalaron's original K16 Cauchy data. Its p8:cor:v2136-pb16-closed (9499–9529) distinguishes owner closure from the conditional claim Q_part^direct=Q_∂ and ρ_part^direct(16)=ρ_∂,16: those equalities consume action-jet data. The source explicitly revisits the distinction in VI-A remark 9532–9541 and the detailed D19 calculation at 5875–5901. The older historical status remark at 9404–9426 says OPEN_OWNER/MAP; the later 9434–9529 theorem updates the direct owner status, while retaining the separate stress certificate. Do not quote the older historical status as current owner status, or quote the later owner closure as unconditional stress closure.

Concrete remaining downstream obligation, not a newly certified II-A gap: On the same generated field/metric/cell chart, establish the exact pullback of the complete action (including projector/field-chart derivatives) or show that after matching quadratic Hessians and first field jets the remaining c(g,η) has D_g c=D_η c=0. This is the source's required bridge from owned response to complete physical stress/work. The inspected II-A corollary correctly leaves that bridge to an independent theorem; thus it is not a missing edge in its negative conclusion.

Batch 25 — Generated-member / owner-coordinate handoff (9 November 2026)

145. Frozen target, exact claim and status

Current authoritative source: Paper2A-GeometricConstitution-v3_206.tex, framework Paper00-PapersMinimal-v2_358, 12,149 lines, SHA-256 167aec2ef3b1c3f30859ca65cf430778440887466a4451d47e6702af0edb9be0. Audit source unchanged from the prior batch.

Target theorem: p2a:thm:v3109-generated-member-owner-corealization (11102–11147). If an occurring root P produces a result-independent, frozen, nonredundant, licensed tuple Q=(q_1,...,q_N); an independently occurring physical owner O already has a declared typed insertion iota_O:Q->T; and on the relevant joint constructor domain its image is a required nonredundant output of this same physical owner, then Occ(P) AND Occ(O) -> CoReal_T(iota_O(Q)|O). The already generated constituents cannot be independently deleted, renormalized, retyped or rebound while claiming the unchanged frozen presentation. This is not a claim that the mere existence of P, O, or a mathematical map creates the insertion or the physical owner.

Status: VERIFIED-CONDITIONAL, at the exact stated joint licensed/GP1-GP2-realizable constructor interface. No additional physical occurrence token, member selection, or GP4 is used.

Related corollary: p2a:cor:v3109-finite-pair-cell-specialization (11149–11170). An occurring finite quadratic owner with predeclared fermionic pair coordinate, and an occurring rooted constructor supplying the specific nonzero Q_Delta=(Delta*,Delta*†) in that coordinate, co-realize the exact Hermitian pairing cell and its fixed action. The source does not claim that any arbitrary matrix is a physical fermionic pairing coefficient.

Related remark: p2a:rem:v3109-source-certificate-relation (11172–11179), expressly excludes resolution of an independently unconstituted owner family. Status: VALID SCOPE LIMIT.

Proof replay: occurrence of rooted P plus the frozen tuple word retains Q in its declared generated sort by v3.101. On a compatible GP1/GP2-exact joint constructor branch, the independently occurring owner O already has the typed map and required output iota_O(Q). Since all typed inputs are retained, no-idle GP2 exactness forces the image in T as a descendant of the same owner. If an output constituent is deleted, rescaled or rebound, the complete frozen owner presentation changes, so it is a different candidate/constructor, not the original licensed handoff. Neither proof nor conclusion constructs the previously absent physical owner, typed map or the occurrence of arbitrary members of a role.

Important quantifier/typing note: The displayed Occ(P) AND Occ(O) implication is read under the theorem's preceding hypotheses, including the requirement that the specific input pair lies in the licensed joint constructor domain. Two marginal occurrences plus an abstract map do not themselves prove that joint admissibility. The phrase "on the joint constructor domain" already states the restriction; spelling it out as JointAdm(P,O) in the box would improve unambiguous use, but this batch does not certify a new gap in the conditional theorem. This is a scope clarification, not an invented compatibility postulate.

147. E3 — Direct finite mathematical reconstruction

Take a finite typed rooted output Q=(2,3) and an owner with already declared identity insertion into a 2-coordinate target. The generated required image is (2,3). Deleting either constituent, changing one coefficient, or swapping target coordinates changes the frozen input or output presentation. GP2 exactness therefore preserves the declared tuple and typed insertion with no additional normalization mark.

For a distinct (still licensed) noninjective insertion iota(q1,q2)=q1+q2, tuples (2,3) and (4,1) give the same scalar image. Thus image-level equality need not determine constituent values or make one source tuple redundant: provenance remains in the frozen Q, while the owner consumes only the predeclared target. If a later physical claim requires reconstruction of constituents, it must import a separate faithfulness/observability certificate. The theorem does not claim such inversion.

Joint-domain stress: Let the finite root and finite owner individually occur, but the typed joint-incidence relation be empty. Then their marginal occurrences alone cannot activate a constructor conditional on that joint incidence. This is outside the theorem's admitted joint constructor domain, and demonstrates why no unconditional Occ(P) AND Occ(O) to arbitrary cross-owner binding is legitimate.

148. E4 — Exact fermionic CAR controls and pair-cell scope

For two fermionic modes obeying CAR, let a_1,a_2 be the exact 4×4 Jordan–Wigner annihilation matrices and set

H_pair(Delta) = (sum_{ij} a_i† Delta_ij a_j† + sum_{ij} a_i (Delta†)_ij a_j)/2.

Then H_pair(Delta)†=H_pair(Delta). Since a_i† a_j† = -a_j† a_i†, only the antisymmetric coefficient (Delta-Delta^T)/2 contributes. In particular a nonzero symmetric matrix gives zero pairing operator, while Delta = [[0,2],[-2,0]] gives a nonzero Hermitian operator and a different fixed action under a factor-three rescaling.

This tests a real interpretation limit, not a counterexample to the paper's downstream physical pair theorem: Paper IV-B's directly citing p4:cor:v4101-generated-pair-member-handoff (5173–5212) explicitly requires Delta* in Lambda² K*, Delta*^T=-Delta*, Delta*!=0, and a licensed owner-preserving support map. That certificate rules out the symmetric-kernel toy model. Paper V-A (3504–3568) also posits a faithful support insertion for its specific rooted neutral cell; this audit traces that direct use, not a global proof of its physical K16 closure.

Reproducible E4 script: Proofs/Paper2A-GeneratedMemberBatch25-v1_1-verify.py; companion ...-results.json; 31/31 exact finite/symbolic controls passed. These tests cover mathematical examples, not GP1/GP2 physical admissibility or the existence of the independent owner, map, and rooted output.

The independently re-executed valid F97 specialization has word-span ranks at degrees 0..7 of [1, 5, 21, 69, 124, 136, 137, 137]. Batch 28 — Finite visible response without a dark-complement selector (9 November 2026)

162. Exact theorem and E1 source freeze

Paper II-A: p2a:cor:v227-visible-closure, 11271–11284; immediately following scope clause 11285–11290. Imported Paper I-B: p1b:thm:v2210-lit-finite-weyl 18949–18988; p1b:thm:response-minimal-visible-realization 18995–19053; p1b:thm:v227-finite-response 22122–22158; p1b:prop:v227-sharp-sample-count 22160–22186; p1b:cor:v227-response-jets 22188–22213; p1b:cor:v227-zero-mode-comparison 22227–22240.

The actual theorem says that exact equality of two complete Hermitian response matrices at m+1 distinct upper-half-plane energies suffices when both complementary dimensions are at most m, under a shared retained/source target and common kinetic/energy normalization. An already licensed pole/readout depending only on that response is consequently common without selecting an unused dark complementary block. It does not construct the actual parent, ports, physics action or detector.

163. E3 finite-polynomial proof and sharpness

Let d_i(z)=det(C_i-zI), N=m_1+m_2, and P(z)=d_1(z)d_2(z)(M_1(z)-M_2(z)), where M_i(z)=zI-A_i+B_i(C_i-zI)^(-1)B_i^*. The zI terms cancel and deg P <= N; Hermiticity gives P(conj z)^* = P(z). Each upper-half-plane exact zero supplies a lower-half-plane zero, hence 2 s > N samples make P the zero matrix polynomial. The source formula s=floor(N/2)+1 follows, with the safe bound m+1 when m_i<=m. The lower bound is not an arbitrary numerical fit: the Paper I-B positive Hermitian sharp witness agrees at i and 2i, differs at 3i when m_1=m_2=2.

164. E3 visible/dark classification and conditional readout

The exact response fixes A, visible pole locations and positive coefficient matrices R_lambda=BP_lambda B^*; the minimal visible environment has complex dimension sum rank R_lambda and is unique up to a unitary fixing the retained frame. A reducing uncoupled dark complement is invisible only to that response: no selection of its internal eigenvalues is needed for response-only readouts. Other source ports, heat/action traces, absolute gravity, metric jets and physical owner may inspect it and require independent certified information.

165. E3/E4 source-jet and adverse controls

For C^k real-source families on fixed finite carriers, sampled equality of source jets to order k at s_k=floor((k+1)(m_1+m_2)/2)+1 fixed common upper-half-plane energies determines those jet functions of spectral energy at that source point; it is not global curvature/source equality. Upper-half-plane tests permit complementary zero modes, whereas coupled zero modes remain in real-zero calculations. Scalar contractions or partial native coefficient tests do not substitute for full retained/source matrix equality. A common frame and exact, not approximate, samples are required. The accompanying executable SymPy script independently passes 30/30 exact controls.

166. Backward dependencies, claim boundary, downstream scope

p1b:thm:v2210-lit-finite-weyl supplies the actual Hermitian block-response definition; p1b:thm:v227-finite-response supplies the finite certificate; p1b:thm:response-minimal-visible-realization supplies the unique visible/dark decomposition. The sharpness proposition is a counter-shortcut control, not an input assumption. The jets corollary has extra differentiability and frame guards. No reference cycle was found. Direct source-checked consumers in III-A 5286–5303, III-D 2855–2871, III-F 20702–20726, V-B 8615–8632 and VI-A 17040–17050 retain the owner/source limitation; the entire charged external/dual/remainder return is not certified by this sampling theorem. None of the four existing II-A gap issues is a material premise of the finite Hermitian polynomial argument.

Batch 29 — Action incidence and observable-minimal certification (9 November 2026)

169. E1 target and proof status

The current source defines the schema

[ \mathsf{ActCert}{P\to\mathcal O} =(S\star,\iota_{P\to\mathcal O},\mathscr V_{\mathcal O}S_\star,\mathcal R_{\mathcal O},\operatorname{Read}_{\mathcal O}). ]

This has five independent typed slots. The source explicitly requires:

  1. Occurrence of geometric provider and observable-facing physical owner (11299–11309).
  2. The preterminal insertion is generated/licensed independently of the terminal values (11309–11310).
  3. The actual action word retains its oriented supports, coefficients, mixed interior terms, kinetics, connections and boundary contacts as required by the specific sort (11310–11313).
  4. Variation and reduction use the order dictated by that same action (11313–11314).
  5. The readout follows on a declared domain (11314–11316).

Definition 11215–11216 expressly denies that introducing the schema makes a constructor exist. The proposition at 11321–11329 is correctly not an existence statement: neither a nonzero provider, nonempty Hom-space, candidate action, nor terminal scalar match by itself discharges the five slots. The positive clause requires the full certificate together with the relevant PCAD, preterminal-preservation and source-incidence hypotheses. Its conclusion is generated descendant readout without a second physical token, not the appearance of a previously nonoccurring owner.

This resolves the relevant actual question: what observable must be explained, and does its already licensed action consume the required source geometry? It does not demand reproducing an unrelated conventional Lagrangian mechanism. No new GP or physical object is created.

B29-E3. Conditional positive proof

Let the exact frozen origin word construct the actual action and its insertion from jointly occurring input roles. If every certificate slot is satisfied, then (i) the preterminal typed relation is retained through the descended map by PCAD; (ii) the same action's complete cross returns are retained by the v3102 theorem, including the declared coefficient and contact terms; (iii) the already constituted owner supplies the variation/reduction domain and declared observable map; (iv) the existing generated-image rule gives the output as a descendant of that owner. GP2 does not require a new occurrence token for such an already-required descendant. The conclusion is conditional on the five-slot certificate; none is generated by this reasoning.

B29-E3. Negative direction and adversarial examples

A nonzero provider in a nonzero vector space permits a zero linear insertion. In a finite two-dimensional control, P=(1,2) and iota=0 give iota(P)=0. Thus the port's nonzero norm proves no nonzero source value or action insertion. An unchosen nonzero morphism may exist in the Hom-space simultaneously; mathematical availability does not certify that its morphism is licensed by the physical owner.

The matrices A=diag(1,0) and B=diag(0,1) have identical scalar trace 1, but distinct source matrix elements. Likewise the two-channel tuples (1,1) and (1,-1) have identical separate absolute-value readouts yet coherent returns 4 and 0. These are mathematical witnesses to the failure of terminal readout to determine a preterminal relation. They are not claimed as counterexamples inside a GP2-admissible physical comparison class.

For S(x,z;g)=1/2(5+g)x²+2xz+3z²/2+g², stationarity gives z*=-2x/3 and

[ S_{\rm red}=\tfrac12(5+g-4/3)x^2+g^2,\qquad \chi_{\rm red}=5+g-4/3. ]

Omitting the mixed term would incorrectly give 5+g; omitting the field-independent g² leaves the field Hessian unchanged but alters the metric/source variation by 2g. That shows why action-level and readout-level agreement are different. For fermionic source normalization, M=Z_L^{-1/2}(\delta^2 S/\delta\bar\psi_L\delta\psi_R)Z_R^{-1/2} must retain orientation and the right word; the finite off-diagonal example gives unequal answers when left/right dressings are exchanged. These calculations test mathematical validity only, not physical action ownership.

B29-E1/E3. Three application targets are interfaces, not certified realizations

Paper II-A lines 11343–11362 lists three possible consumers: mixed charged-fermion derivatives, regular stationary curvature Schur response and K16 restriction S_16,normal=R_16(S_F,complete). Their formulas are mathematically valid on their declared regular/action domains. They do not establish that the required owners, chiral incidence, operator inverse, boundary contacts or physical readouts occur in each application.

  • Paper V-B Paper5B-TerminalSpectrum-v5_187.tex 4683–4689 and 6509–6515 distinguishes mathematically constant family data and native geometric candidates from actual mixed action source/loop assignment.
  • Paper III-G Paper3G-TechnicalMaterial-v1_44.tex 8658–8667 cites the observable-minimal route, while explicitly withholding action certification, Newton readout and neglected higher cumulants.

These checks are source-aligned direct uses, not full independent proofs of downstream physical theorems. There is no direct other-paper citation to p2a:prop:v229-action-consumer-firewall in the frozen v2.358 minimal archive.

B29-E3. Adjacent observable-minimal result

The corollary at 11368–11391 has two alternative sufficient routes for licensed r=tilde r∘F:

  • Family route: an actual generated output X is shown to lie in nonempty licensed family 𝔛; F is constant on that family. Then r(X) is fixed without selecting X.
  • Direct action-invariant route: the actual action contraction itself determines F(X), even if a complete operator reconstruction is unavailable. Then apply tilde r.

The two routes are OR, not two required independent postulates. Both retain the action-origin premise: neither permits replacing a physical action by a geometrically available comparison family. These statements are direct consequences of p2a:cor:v228-constant-source-fibre, p2a:thm:v3102-preterminal-relation-preservation and factorization.

The polynomial certificate proposition at 11393–11413 gives two separately sufficient real-algebraic checks on an already licensed nonempty real family C_j(D,z)=0 for a licensed polynomial observable O:

[ d^N\bigl(O-o(D)\bigr)=\sum_jh_j C_j\ (d\ne0),\quad\text{or}\quad \bigl(O-o(D)\bigr)^{2k}+\sum_\ell s_\ell^2=\sum_jh_jC_j. ]

The ideal-membership route uses the nonzero divisor d on the admitted domain. The SOS route uses real nonnegativity; it does not hold over arbitrary complex variables. Neither proves that a polynomial certificate of bounded degree always exists. The proposition correctly retains all these conditions and does not license the readout or action.

Upstream: p2a:cor:v239-observable-minimal-action (11368–11391) is the motivating consumer and preserves actual-output/action-origin guards. p2a:def:v229-action-incidence-certificate (11299–11317) is required to apply any mathematical certificate to the actual charged action, not a premise of the purely mathematical trace identity. The anchored heat theorem imports the quantitative Bregman theorem at its last sentence; the normalized determinant proposition independently imports the anchored row domain and Hadamard. p2a:prop:v267-unmarked-heat-no-converse is a negative boundary control for the rooted theorem, not a missing assumption. No new G0–GP3 principle.

E3 independent proof: matrix heat–Bregman

For X=B-D, H(u)=D+uX, define f(u)=Tr exp(-τH(u)). Twice differentiating the trace yields

f''(u)=τ²∫₀¹ Tr[X exp(-τ(1-v)H(u)) X exp(-τvH(u))] dv.

In an orthonormal eigenbasis of H(u), the integrand is Σ_{ij}|X_ij|² exp(-τ[(1-v)h_i+v h_j]); each coefficient is positive. Thus, if X≠0, f''(u)>0 at every u, and Taylor's integral remainder gives Hτ(B,D)=∫₀¹(1-u)f''(u)du>0. If the two endpoints lie in the order interval [0,LI], all eigenvalues of H(u) lie there and every weight lies in [exp(-τL),1]. This proves the two-sided HS bound without assuming simultaneous diagonalizability. For several sectors, a strictly positive weight sum of nonnegative gaps vanishes only sectorwise.

E4 stress tests: B=[[1,1],[1,1]], D=diag(2,0) are distinct, noncommuting and isospectral, so all unmarked traces agree, but H_{ln 2}(B,D)=(3/4)ln2>0. The upper quadratic estimate is not global when the order-box premise is omitted: scalar B=-1,D=0,τ=1 gives e−2>1/2. This is a scope validation, not a flaw in the source (which states the order box).

E3 independent proof: tagged Euclidean frequency

For spectral measure μ_g of a normalized vector, d=∫x dμ_g and Rω=∫1/(ω²+x)dμ_g. Use the rational identity

(d-x)/(ω²+x) = (d-x)/(ω²+d) + (d-x)²/[(ω²+d)(ω²+x)].

Integrating eliminates the first term and yields the stated positive gap. Because (ω²+B)^{-1}>0, equality iff (B-d)g=0. On the cyclic tagged vector this gives 〈g,(z²−B)^{-1}g〉=1/(z²−d) for all nonpole z. No claim is made about the other left directions or right-sector coupling. Example B=diag(2,10) and B'=diag(2,100) have the same complete g=e₁ tagged response and different full matrices. This tagged free calculation is not an interacting pole certificate.

E3 independent proof: rooted heat and determinant

Given a complete rooted ON left basis, D=Σλ_r|g_r〉〈g_r| and independent moments 〈g_r,Bg_r〉=λ_r, scalar Jensen for the spectral measure of each g_r gives 〈g_r,e^(−τB)g_r〉≥exp(−τλ_r); strictness forces Bg_r=λ_r g_r. Summation over a complete ON frame makes equality iff B=D. The linear Bregman correction vanishes because each rooted diagonal element of B-D vanishes, so the quantitative Bregman bound transfers exactly. For an anchored rank-one-row packet M=Σ√λ_r g_r r_r†, with ||r_r||=1, M†g_r=√λ_r r_r, verifying the first moments. The Gram G_{rs}=〈r_r,r_s〉 has unit diagonal; B=Λ^{1/2}GΛ^{1/2}; Hadamard gives det B≤det Λ, equality iff all right rays are orthonormal. Positive λ_r matters for this iff.

Example M=[[1,0],[6/5,8/5]] yields B=[[1,6/5],[6/5,4]], with D=diag(1,4), exact moments and det B=64/25<4; rooted heat difference is strictly positive. Completeness cannot be dropped: on an incomplete rooted frame, untested block(s) can vary without changing the marked observations.

E3 independent proof: exact relation-fibre criterion

For real polynomial constraints C_j(x)=0, the ideal J=<C(x),C(y),q(x)-q(y)> defines exactly the same-data pairs. O factors through q as a set map on q(F) iff O_i(x)-O_i(y) vanishes on all real zeros of J. By the real Nullstellensatz, this is equivalent to membership in sqrt_R(J) for each component. It is not the complex radical and does not assert that the descended set map is polynomial or analytic. For q=x², O=x⁴ factors (difference divisible by x²-y²); O=x does not (compare x=1 and y=-1).

E4 negative unmarked trace example and direct consumer

Take D=diag(1,2,4) and an orthogonal L rotating the 1st and 3rd eigenvectors with sin φ=4/5. Then B=LDL†≠D shares every unmarked heat trace with D but Tr(D²)-Tr(DB)=144/25. Thus a single unmarked heat value alone never proves rooted alignment on the general domain; the source expressly contains this counter-boundary.

Direct downstream source uses (limited, not global validation): Paper3F-CompleteDynamics-v1_253.tex 11828–11855 imports the heat–Bregman test for the actual same-action finite charged source if constituted; 11857–11879 imports the rooted heat/determinant alternatives with independently verified first moments and anchored right rows. The scalar Euclidean heat extraction at 11881–11908 explicitly demands the same physical finite chiral action source, normalization and removal of extra contributions. No other external paper proof is declared fully audited by this Batch 30.

Random cases are corroborative, not substitutes for the analytic E3 proofs.

The source does not require a separately certified universal full-parent Lie map for these finite conditional claims. No material route was found from any of the four inherited audit issues to the inspected finite Petersen incidence theorem.

3. Independent E3 group proof

Set G=A_5, acting on Ω={1,2,3,4,5}. The subgroup A_{4,i}={g∈A_5:g(i)=i} has order 60/5=12. The five stabilizers are distinct and conjugate. For i≠j, fixing both labels permits only even permutations of the remaining three labels, hence A_{4,i}∩A_{4,j}≅C_3, order three. Every triple stabilizer intersects in the identity. The inverse image under 2I→A_5 of each order-three subgroup has order six and is cyclic: any central C_2 extension of C_3 is cyclic of order six. Thus H_i≅2T, H_i∩H_j≅C_6, and the ten axes are distinct, with pairwise intersection precisely the center.

The ten unordered two-subsets of Ω are the ten Petersen vertices. Two vertices are adjacent iff their two-label subsets are disjoint. Each has C(3,2)=3 neighbors; there are 10×3/2=15 edges. A_5 acts transitively on points, two-subsets, and unordered disjoint two-subset pairs. Their stabilizer orders are respectively 60/5=12, 60/10=6, and 60/15=4. The kernel of the 2I action is the common central C_2, as asserted.

A 2T support has exactly one subgroup of order 24 (itself). The projective S_4 of 2O has only one A_4 subgroup, because any subgroup of order 12 is index two and hence normal, and the sign homomorphism gives its unique index-two subgroup. The quotient A_5 of 2I has five A_4 subgroups, namely the point stabilizers. Thus among already-listed candidate types [2T],[2O],[2I], only [2I] meets the independently frozen five-core incidence. Nothing here produces the requirement or the physical occurrence of that candidate.

4. Independent E4 exact certificate

The executable Proofs/Paper2A-PetersenBatch31-v1_1-verify.py enumerates A_5 from parity-even permutations, point-stabilizer cosets, pair intersections, 10-vertex graph adjacency and 15 edges. It verifies the action orbits and stabilizer sizes, the exact Petersen matrix identity A²=2I−A+J and spectrum (3,1^5,−2^4), the core incidence map rank 5 and quadrupole projector rank 5, and the graph Hodge edge-cycle dimensions 9+6=15. It separately reconstructs the ten published Q(√5) projective axis coordinates of Paper I-A 3757–3769, their two exact squared overlaps 5/9 and 1/9, the tight four-axis core frames and rank-five quadrupole Gram G=(4/3)P_5. The group-classification boundary and nonconverse are also checked. 39/39 named exact checks passed. Arithmetic is exact (sympy, Python integer/permutation enumeration); no numerical tolerance or fitted physics is used. The script and full named results are included in the successor archive.

5. Adversarial boundary checks

  • Unconditional-support trap: a generic S^3, an existing SU(2) parent, or the 15-dimensional H_1 seed does not by itself generate a finite embedded 2I support. The theorem assumes Supp_{2I} has already occurred. This is not a missing premise in a conditional theorem.
  • Circular five-core selector trap: declaring the required five-core relation because 2I happens to support it would not meet the frozen-role premise of the corollary; that route is excluded by the source itself (11647–11650).
  • Abstract-vs-physical 15 trap: fifteen Petersen edges, fifteen harmonic components, fifteen group-induced operators, and fifteen physical particles are distinct sorts; no type identification is proved by dimension alone.
  • Mark/effect trap: the whole unmarked orbit is mathematically generated; no one H_i, C_6 sign, edge orientation, preparation or effect becomes a G0-occurring token.
  • Aut(Petersen) trap: the full abstract relabeling group is S_5, but the inner action from the supplied 2I factors only through A_5.
  • External physics trap: the finite module proof does not establish a gauge coupling, mass coefficient, particle spectrum, or measured probability.

3. Independent mathematical replay (E3)

Put t_i=-iσ_i/2 in su(2), with [t_i,t_j]=ε_{ijk}t_k. The adjoint Killing form in this basis is B(t_i,t_j)=-2δ_ij. A bi-invariant metric on a round embedded radius-R three-sphere satisfies g_R(t_i,t_j)=R²δ_ij/4=-(R²/8)B(t_i,t_j). Hence the metric is indeed a single positive Killing ray. The invariant symmetric bilinear form equations ad(t_i)^T G+G ad(t_i)=0 have a one-dimensional solution space G=aI, a>0; there are no additional isotropy-invariant shape moduli.

For e_i=2t_i/R, the orthonormal-frame brackets are [e_i,e_j]=(2/R)ε_{ijk}e_k. The Levi-Civita connection of the bi-invariant metric is ∇_{e_i}e_j = 1/2[e_i,e_j]. Therefore every orthonormal two-plane has sectional curvature

K(e_i,e_j)=(1/4)||[e_i,e_j]||²=1/R², i≠j.

Since the dimension is three, Scal=2∑_{i<j}K(e_i,e_j)=6/R² and Ric=(2/R²)g. Under R→λR, Scal→λ^{-2}Scal, so K_p R²=6 is invariant. The scalar-curvature length is ell_K=K_p^{-1/2}=R/√6; it is not numerically the sphere radius. This proves the corollary's mathematical content independently of its source table.

There is no radius selector in the result: the map R∈(0,∞)↔K_p=6/R²∈(0,∞) is one-to-one, and the whole positive ray remains. The source's statement that a later generated dimensionful curvature standard may actualize the ray is explicitly conditional, not itself a G0/GP selector.

4. Normalization-inheritance firewall and bounded downstream trace

Paper II-A p2:thm:gp2-generated-owner-normalization-inheritance, lines 3250–3290, requires: a prior constituted owner, generated parent relation and normalization in a declared typed slot, a candidate-independent, host-natural, already licensed same-sort constructor, and an absence of a separately generated normalization-deforming endomorphism. Its proof shows that freely changing an inherited normalized slot violates GP2 exactness/no-insertion unless the deformation is already generated. Thus equality under an identity owner transport is supported, while equality between independent owner scales is not.

The distinction is materially consumed downstream:

  • Paper III-E Paper3E-GravityAction-v1_187.tex, lines 455–482, defines an additional momentum-to-EH cell comparison residual epsilon_{K→U}=log(U/(rho_H K_p)), with rho_H=3/π. It explicitly says U=(3/π)K_p is conditional on the evaluated CELL_{K→U} comparison, not on dimensionality or the scalarizer alone.
  • Paper VI-A Paper6A-GravityCosmology-v2_250.tex, lines 106–128, retains the same condition for every momentum-radius rewrite. Paper VI-B Paper6B-CosmologyReadouts-v1_196.tex, lines 299–307, likewise distinguishes intrinsic K_p from the additional cell test.

These are bounded source-alignments, not an independent audit of the full CELL_{K→U} physical test or EH scalar action. Nothing here proves that residual vanishes, nor asserts equality with coefficient one.

5. Early finite grammar and physical-domain guards

  • Finite presentation (p2:def:finite-presentation-convention): the finite inventory counts generator schemata, not individual elements in an indexed family. A single schema parameterized by an already constituted continuum remains one schema. Therefore finite presentation must not be replaced by finitely many mathematical parameter values, and it cannot be used as an absolute finite ontic-carrier selector outside its stated role.
  • Structured candidate (p2:def:structured-role-realization): the complete candidate-internal presentation Pres_M includes types and provenance of generated items. Internal candidate self-data are not globally frozen before GP3; operation and object-carrier retention are distinct. In the source, the finite faithful state-affine representation claim at lines 921–927 is sector-qualified. It is not a proof that every Adm_R topological parent acts faithfully on a finite homogeneous state span. Consequently it does not discharge the existing BH-IIA-PORT-001 issue and does not reopen the independently verified primitive endpoint skeleton route.
  • Finite state/effect convention (p2:def:standard-finite-state-space): for a fixed finite complex C* algebra A=⊕ M_{dλ}(C), the state space is all normalized positive complex-linear functionals, effects satisfy 0≤E≤1, and a∈A_sa evaluates by ω(a). The previous Batch 7 state–test theorem proves canonical block-density representations within this grammar. Neither the definition nor that proof establishes physical preparation/performance for every state/effect/outcome.
  • Complex scope (p2a:rem:complex-state-grammar-scope): the source explicitly refrains from claiming that the complex C* grammar is uniquely forced by GP1–GP3 among real, quaternionic or general Jordan alternatives. This is a deliberate candidate-domain boundary, not a failure of the finite complex-state algebraic proof.

6. Independent controls and limits (E4)

The local executable Paper2A-CurvatureBackfillBatch32-v1_1-verify.py performs 29/29 exact SymPy checks (no tolerance/fitting): su(2) structure constants and Killing form, invariant metric-ray dimension, round curvature/Ricci/scalar, radius and curvature-length transformations, six exact radius examples, dimensional cross-owner coefficient freedom, plus finite C⊕M₂(C) state/effect normalization examples and the non-finite cardinality of a continuum-indexed one-schema family. These are mathematical/semantic controls, not actual physical curvature calibration, finite parent occurrence, or state preparation experiments.

Adversarially, replacing K_p=6/R² by 1/R² confuses scalar curvature with sectional curvature. Treating ell_K=R confuses scalar-curvature length with geometric radius. Setting U=K_p from dimension matching alone violates the cross-owner proof guard. Replacing a finite presentation by a finite set of generated values is a definitional misreading. Treating an arbitrary C* state as an occurring preparation is a physical-domain leap. Treating a sector-qualified faithful operation port as universal would silently reintroduce BH-IIA-PORT-001. None of those stronger inferences is asserted by the six source-order items.

B33-2. Exact geometric verification

Let M be a smooth manifold. For X∈Γ(TM), define δ_X f=X(f). Then the differential operator obeys the Leibniz identity δ_X(fg)=δ_X(f)g+fδ_X(g), and the vector-field commutator obeys [δ_X,δ_Y]=δ_[X,Y]. For finite-dimensional smooth manifolds, local coordinate functions separate vector fields, so δ is injective as a mathematical map into derivations. None of these mathematical statements implies that the codomain action is already generated as a physical operation: the source requires a frozen typed action constructor.

The momentum-seed example in the source has stronger, checkable content. On the round S³(R)⊂R⁴, let x_i be the degree-one coordinate harmonic and V_i=∇x_i the intrinsic gradient. In ambient coordinates V_i=∂_i-(x_i/R²)∑_k x_k ∂_k along the sphere. Therefore

[V_i,V_j]=(x_i ∂_j-x_j ∂_i)/R².

These six brackets for i<j are linearly independent global Killing sections and span the six-dimensional so(4) action. Thus the generated vector-field image is finite-dimensional as a mathematical subspace, whereas Γ(TS³) remains infinite-dimensional. The E4 certificate verifies all 6×4=24 bracket coordinate identities, six induced derivation identities, the six-section rank, and the Leibniz rules. This verifies the mathematical feasibility of finite generated images without equating them to the entire smooth parent carrier or certifying physical actuality.

An independent S¹ check takes X=sinθ∂θ, Y=cosθ∂θ, giving [X,Y]=-∂θ. It independently verifies the induced derivation commutator identity and product rule.

B33-3. G0 logic and the minimal-formulation compatibility question

The source defines, relative to a frozen presentation P_n, the new frontier Δ_q and the closure P_n[q] (lines 1261–1277). Its displayed G0 predicate (lines 1278–1292) reads

PhysOcc_{P_n}(q) ↔ ∃E [Actual_E(q) ∧ GeomLic_{P_n→P_n[q]}(q;E) ∧ InvDist_{P_n[q]}(q;E)].

The existential atom Actual_E(q) is an independent primitive contingent-token predicate (lines 1293–1295). Therefore the rule does not infer actuality from membership in a mathematically/structurally admissible family. The source explicitly states Adm_{P_n}(q) ↛ PhysOcc_{P_n}(q) (lines 1320–1331). Claim-relative InvDist distinguishes only the alternatives asserted by the particular claim; it does not silently require complete carrier tomography. All 8 Boolean conjunction cases and a three-token nontomographic comparison are checked in the local certificate.

Precise formulation compatibility: the user's intended minimalist G0 sentence, ∃G (nontrivial(G) ∧ complete(G) ∧ geometry(G)), is not literally the same logical formula as the source's schema defining which role-typed tokens count as physical occurrences. The former is an existence statement, the latter an occurrence-qualification/typing semantics. A single existential geometry witness by itself cannot entail universal licensing conditions for every proposed q. But the source uses the extra clauses as definitions of physical occurrence and role constitution, not as a theorem claiming to derive new geometric roots from nothing. Consequently we record G0-FORMULATION-COMPAT-B33 as a compatibility task, not a new failed theorem edge or a fifth physical principle. Reconciliation should explicitly separate (i) a single actual nontrivial complete geometric root, (ii) definitions of typed physical occurrence, and (iii) GP1–GP3 generated role descendants. Do not retroactively assert a logical equivalence that the present source has not proved.

The strong primitive-role predicate Geo_R is constituted later, lines 6290–6341: connected local reapplication, effectiveness, and the order-swap comparison are part of the claimed role's frozen inventory, not free labels or theorems of bare set existence. Source claims about their selected least skeleton remain within that existing role domain. This batch neither derives a new universal role-occurrence theorem nor challenges the previously audited S¹/S³ parent selection or m_p=1.

B33-4. Instantiational / constitutive split, co-realization and no duplicate witnesses

If Δ_q=∅, the presentation stays P_n. The token still requires Actual_E(q) for a particular occurrence; prior role eligibility cannot create it. If Δ_q≠∅, the new comparison-bearing role is sent through relative GP1–GP3 completion preserving P_n. The existence of a completion is itself needed for admissibility; the displayed definition of P_n[q] does not prove closure is nonobstructed for arbitrary new requests. The distinction is a source-declared boundary, not a hidden gap in its case-split definition.

For a root P with frozen finite generated-constituent presentation P=G_Σ(r_1,...,r_N), GP2 no-omission entails that every nonredundant required r_i occurs as a constituent inside the same root witness (lines 2045–2081). A separately named preparation or measurement, or a mathematically available but unlicensed operation diagram, has no such inference. The generated-role theorem (2104–2136) and operational momentum corollary (2676–2714) provide the corresponding role-level chain; the latter does not license a numerical momentum measurement or unit calibration.

For the tangent-section image, the consumed lemma (3160–3194) requires a frozen host-natural F:X→Y, canonical invariant nonredundant im F, and GP1-finite presentation. GP2 retains that image in the object sort. A canonical induced derivation on it is a distinct operation-typed output and co-realizes only if licensed. No autonomous role is created merely because the object and operation have two names.

1. Frozen source-level targets and verdicts

Named target Current TeX lines Source-as-printed claim / domain Adjudication

1.1 Definitional precision: atomicity is not primitive physical occurrence

For a frozen commutative type monoid M_Σ, a is atomic when a≠0 and every decomposition a=b+c has a zero summand. This is a type predicate on the already generated monoid. It cannot declare a new physical root, infer smallest dimension, or classify an after-the-fact chosen representation as primitive. For the free commutative monoid N² with generators e₁,e₂, e₁ and e₂ are atoms, while e₁+e₂ is decomposable. Constructor length ℓ(i,j)=i+j gives finite sublevels of cardinality (N+1)(N+2)/2, invariant under the unmarked swap e₁↔e₂. These are existence examples, not a framework selection of N.

1.2 Capacity, occupation and family bounds are distinct GP1 clauses

A one-dimensional one-particle space still has dim Symⁿ(C)=1 for every n, hence ⊕_{n≥0} Symⁿ(C) is infinite-dimensional without a constituted occupation cutoff. GP1 explicitly requires finite total retained composition/occupation capacity; it does not infer it from one-particle finiteness.

Likewise the family H_n=nH, for a fixed nonzero 2×2 Hermitian H, is unbounded in operation norm even though every H_n acts on the same finite carrier. Under GP1 FRC-b it is inadmissible if the full unbounded family is constituted as a physical datum; finitely many chosen operations or a symbolic family have different typed scope. FRC-b is not a universal bound on all mathematically expressible multiples of H.

A GP1 obstruction is a negative admissibility judgment. The source's GP2 generated-output and Paper III-A continuation requirements are separately needed for any positive successor process. Nothing in the target remark asserts that a violating candidate undergoes a physically occurring transition.

3. Independent E3 proof — GP1-TB

Let A be a finite-dimensional unital complex C* algebra, S(A) its state space, and ι:X→S(A) an injective realized-state map. The set ι(X) is totally bounded in any finite-dimensional state norm, even when it is not closed. Assume for a comparison metric d on X that

d(x,y) ≤ ω(‖ι(x)−ι(y)‖*) with ω(t)→0 as t→0.

Fix ε>0 and choose δ>0 such that ω(t)<ε whenever 0≤t<δ. A finite δ/2-net in ι(X) can be centered in ι(X) itself; any two points associated to the same center are at norm distance less than δ, so their d distance is less than ε. This yields a finite ε-cover of (X,d) and hence a finite maximal size for every ε-separated set. If d is induced by a uniformly operator-norm-bounded test family T, then

d(x,y)=sup_{a∈T}|(ι(x)−ι(y))(a)| ≤ (sup_{a∈T}‖a‖) ‖ι(x)−ι(y)‖*,

so the modulus holds with ω(t)=Mt. No finite cardinality, minimum positive separation, unique radius, or Lie action follows.

Necessary-hypothesis adversarial control: take infinitely many distinct rational p∈(0,1), embed faithfully as ρ_p=diag(p,1−p) in S(M₂(C)), but impose an unrelated discrete comparison d(p,q)=1 whenever p≠q. This comparison is not totally bounded and has no uniform state-norm comparison modulus. It is outside p2:cor:gp1-tb's hypotheses, not an admissible counterexample to that theorem. Merely having a finite state algebra never supplies the required bridge.

4. Independent E3 boundary analysis

The condition P_gbd(q,N) alone is a support-inclusion statement, not a claim that the realized support literally equals Γ_q^{≤N}. If a source consumer requires all nonredundant generated sublevel items, that stronger completeness follows only when the frozen grammar includes those items and GP2 exactness/no-omission applies, as in lines 3624–3637. The FRC-d remark is correct as a description of this joint boundary-and-exactification mechanism; the inclusion alone cannot carry the whole lower-ideal conclusion.

Every finite support of finite-length elements is below some finite N after a specific intrinsic grammar exists, but this existential mathematical observation does not certify a particular a priori physical boundary, the completeness of the retained sublevel, or candidate-independence of its constructor. The source preserves these distinctions. A chosen cutoff introduced only because it reproduces a desired physical number is not intrinsic under lines 1433–1438.

The bounded direct consumer scan found the following correct guarded uses:

  • Paper II-A 3601–3638: P_gbd restricts exact closure only if registered in the frozen signature.
  • Paper II-A 5982–6000: intrinsic word-budget representation bookkeeping is not automatically the physical tensor-word grammar.
  • Paper III-F 1143–1155: states a later, stage-specific scalar-boundary discharge, not universal GP1 selection.
  • Paper IV-A 4252–4258 and Paper IV-B 1476–1523: finite bosonic occupation support is a GP1 capacity consequence, while retaining a complete standard initial occupation sublevel needs a separately supplied/generated P_gbd(occ,N_max) and GP2 exactification.

These bounded source checks are not a full independent audit of Papers III-F, IV-A or IV-B.

GP1 finite embedding not automatically affine 1698–1709 Correct scope firewall; E4 connected compact counter-control
Master theorem's universal-Lie wording 7815–7825 Existing BH-IIA-PORT-001; not discharged or duplicated by this batch

3. Independent representation-theoretic derivation

Let σ_i be the Pauli matrices. Every qubit state is ρ(r)=1/2(I+r·σ) with |r|≤1. For U∈SU(2), conjugation ρ→UρU† implements a 3D rotation on the Bloch vector. Its kernel is the two-element center {+I,-I}: any matrix commuting with all σ_i is scalar, and det U=1 restricts it to ±I. Thus the faithful operation group is SU(2)/{±I}=SO(3). It is faithful on the 3D real traceless Hermitian test space; the homogeneous affine action is 4D real.

Suppose a continuous complex 2D linear representation of SO(3) existed and were faithful. Pull back to SU(2). Every finite-dimensional complex representation of compact SU(2) is completely reducible. In dimension 2, the only possibilities are two trivial one-dimensional factors or the nontrivial spin-1/2 irrep. The latter maps the central -I to -I₂, so it cannot factor through SO(3). The former is trivial and not faithful. Contradiction. The SO(3) complex vector representation is 3D and faithful; no universal dim(H) bound on a carrier lift follows.

These are statements about the exact operation quotient. Replacing it with SU(2) changes the representation group and is not a carrier lift of the same quotient.

4. Connected compact non-affinity control (E4)

Equip X=S¹ with its usual round metric and define the faithful state embedding j(t)∈S(M₂(C)) through its Bloch vector

r(t)=(cos t, sin t, cos2t)/4.

The vector has norm ≤sqrt(2)/4<1, and the first two coordinates make the map injective. Compactness makes j a homeomorphism onto its image and the inverse uniformly continuous. The round-metric isometries include shifts t→t+θ.

The affine span of the image is spanned by functions {1,cos t,sin t,cos2t}. For θ=π/4, the shifted third coordinate is cos(2t+π/2)=-sin2t, which is linearly independent of that span. Thus there exists no affine A on the 3D state affine span satisfying Aj(t)=j(t+π/4) for all t. The eight-point exact evaluation matrix has rank 4; adjoining the translated third-coordinate values raises rank to 5. By contrast, a π/2 shift of t preserves the span, demonstrating the specific nature of the obstruction.

The control refutes only the invalid inference finite-state embedding + metric isometry ⇒ state-affine action, not the primitive-role theorem: this example is not asserted to realize the complete GP2 primitive-role signature. It also has a compact Lie group of intrinsic isometries in spite of the non-affine embedding, proving that failure of this port does not imply that a group is non-Lie.

  4. Linear readout lemma uses nondegeneracy of (B,T)↦Tr(BT): Tr(E_ij E_kl)=δ_il δ_jk; any B decomposes as a finite sum of rank-one ψ⊗ε. This is an algebraic normal form, not a declaration that every rank-one marked boundary occurs.
  5. The unmarked theorem fixes the exact closing word before using the trace lemma. Paper II-F p2a:lem:repair-natural-functional-coefficients, 2783–2805, is the relevant stronger-alternative guard: a merely unitary-invariant linear functional has form c_H Tr, and normalized trace on every carrier demonstrates failure of uniform coefficient one without licensed sum constraints. 3. Independently derived mathematics (E3)

Scalar and cardinality classification

For f∈End_C(C), linearity forces f(z)=zf(1) and conversely every a∈C defines f_a(z)=az. In one complex dimension dagger is conjugation, positivity is |w|²≥0, 0≤z≤1 gives the effect interval, and unitarity is |z|=1. A further idempotence relation gives only {0,1}, demonstrating that the four display slices are not exhaustive restrictions.

For n=dim_C H>0, End(H)^*≅C^{n²} has cardinal c. A finite-parametrized finite diagram contributes at most c instances; countably many such diagrams remain ≤c. If X is nonempty separable, a continuous f:X→C is determined by its restriction to a countable dense subset: |C(X,C)|≤c^{aleph_0}=c; arbitrary constants give the lower bound. If |X|=c, all (not necessarily continuous) functions have cardinal c^c=2^c. These are cardinality statements, not measured-quantity predictions.

Canonical duality versus arbitrary natural functional

The coevaluation is the identity tensor, so it is basis independent, and the unmarked contraction is Tr exactly. The isomorphism (End H)^*≅End H via the trace pairing is proved using matrix units. A rank-one mark B=ψ⊗ε gives Tr(BT)=ε(Tψ). The same complex number can therefore arise from an unmarked loop or a marked boundary process. Disjointness resides in the frozen diagram provenance, not the numeric codomain C. This is already the distinction in lines 1961–1963 and 1981–1985, so it is not a new gap.

The family λ_H(T)=Tr(T)/dim H is unitary natural, tensor multiplicative and normalized at H=C, but fails unnormalized direct-sum additivity. Thus it does not refute the theorem, whose antecedent is the particular canonical duality word. The separate II-F coefficient lemma correctly classifies the broader family and the need for licensed sum links.

Two types, not two functions

The normal-form theorem divides primitive connected typed diagram occurrences into external-boundary empty and nonempty. Internal loops within a boundary process remain generated in its effective arrow T; a numerically equal loop rewriting with a rank-one insertion does not erase the boundary provenance. Unlicensed tensor/sum/exterior/oriented/entropy/function-calculus decorations cannot be admitted by ambient category mathematics alone. The result does not prove that every physically possible observable is a realized member of FSR_Σ, or that the two role types fix a unique readout law or value.

B. Backward proof slices and the earliest necessary edges

B1. Constituents of one occurring composite

p2:prin:geometric-primacy-minimal-constitution (1243–1366) provides the claim-relative G0 occurrence; the finite presentation definition (2017–2043) freezes the constructor G_Σ, its full list of required nonredundant constituents, and each native sort; GP2's no omission and typed host faithfulness clauses (2906–2929) force their correct simultaneous representation. The proof's inference at 2064–2074 exactly matches those predicates. The no-insertion clause (2895–2904) and the final proof sentences (2075–2080) prohibit claiming an apparatus or independent scalar estimator as an unlicensed extra constituent.

Independent finite-content proof: let R={(port_i, sort_i, value_i)} be the finite set of frozen nonredundant required typed constituents and let Pres(M) be the exact realization's represented typed set. GP2 gives R ⊆ Pres(M) with no deletion or retyping, and no inserted marked content. Therefore every port_i occurs in the correct sort, irrespective of numerical coincidences between different sorts. The implication is about represented generated content, not an Actual_E(r_i) primitive-token predicate. This is not circular: the definition supplies generator and required-output status, whereas the theorem supplies faithful representation in the occurring composite.

The mathematically stronger statement “every diagram that could be made in Σ occurs physically” is false and not claimed. For a G0-occurring P with only frozen (object, operation) ports, a new apparatus diagram or history is not a constituent solely because that diagram is in the ambient mathematical grammar.

B2. Generated-role content

The role-descendant definition 2083–2102 requires more than a finite list: an exact generated role, frozen law/topology and GP1 structured realizability. The existing theorem 2104–2136 co-realizes that role; neither theorem implies actualization of all its values, preparations or measurements. An autonomous additional carrier is not silently inferred; p2:lem:gp2-same-role-no-splitting (3196–3235) is needed when a specific generated finite object is to be internal to an already constituted carrier.

B3. Constructor origin and the explicit obstruction branch

The origin certificate 2197–2230 provides precisely a finite typed path whose inputs, intermediate outputs and target sort were fixed independently of result. Given the roots RootReal_P(D) of 2235–2240, every intermediate step and final q belong to Gen_Σ(D). If there is no GP1-finite GP2-exact faithful candidate, Obs_FRC is the defined obstruction and no physical q is asserted. Otherwise, for every admissible candidate, omission/retyping of q violates GP2 (2260–2277). Crucially, the two branches are alternatives, not an unconditional existence theorem. Later GP3 may compare complete candidates but is not the origin of the output.

This is also the precisely correct treatment of a frozen but unrealizable constructor: it does not prove q physically exists. Nor may an after-the-fact constructor be invented merely because it reproduces q.

B4. No-idle closure

p2:cor:no-idle-licensed-constructor (3049–3081) is an immediate consequence of the no-omission, no-retyping and closure components of the GP2 realization-defect definition (2840–2873) and postulate (2877–3046). It is not a new operator-availability axiom; a mathematically definable but unlicensed operation remains outside Gen_R(M).

C. E3 / E4 verification

E3 independently models a finite frozen port signature as a typed set R and an exact representation as a type-preserving Pres(M). Completeness is forced at every r_i by GP2; numerical equality does not identify incompatible sorts. If no exact realization is available, the source's FRC alternative is required.

E4 replay: the deterministic standard-library script Paper2A-GeneratedConstituentsBatch37-v1_1-verify.py passed 35/35 individual finite logical controls. It checks missing ports, same-number/different-sort collisions, disallowed extra output, unlicensed or post-result constructors, wrong typed roots, absent joint roots, the nonobstruction branch and role-versus-token actualization. These tests do not establish existence of physical composite P, universal GP1 consistency, or physical occurrence of specific members.

Root occurrence normal form. Signature bijection. II-A 2742–2749 gives a complete finite proof: for every r matching image supplies at least one term T; injectivity on T makes it unique. Injectivity on r makes that assignment one-to-one, and equality of images gives surjectivity. Equality of signature components including the role gives type preservation. No probabilistic or physical selector is used. The noninjective case cannot infer unique routing; even existence of a signature-preserving bijection can fail if multiplicity histograms differ despite identical signature sets. This last adversarial observation is a boundary, not a contradiction of the source's positive implication.

GP1/parent regularity. II-A 2762–2798 merely records which previously verified comparison route a later argument actually consumes. p2:cor:gp1-tb (1547–1585) requires an explicit comparison modulus; p2:thm:frc-automatic-compact-lie-closure (1587–1665) is conditional on a faithful affine operation port. Batch 39 — Intrinsic generated defects versus GP2 realization defects

Paper II-A — Batch 39: Intrinsic generated defects and GP2 realization exactness

Audit mode: source-bound single-reviewer replay (E1), independent finite mathematical checks (E3), and explicitly nonphysical adversarial controls (E4). No separate Work agents were used.
Source freeze: Paper00-PapersMinimal-v2_358(1).zip, Paper2A-GeometricConstitution-v3_206.tex (lines below refer to this 12,149-line TeX); current DAG Atlas Paper00-DAGAtlas-v1_53.zip.
Scope: Source-order backfill, primary definitions 2800–2873, GP2 postulate 2875–3047, reused no-idle corollary 3049–3081, distinction remark 3083–3095, and the later rank-one source example 8218–8243. No assertion of full-paper certification.

B39-1 — Exact source-bound claims and statuses (E1)

Label TeX lines Exact target and scope Audit disposition
p2:def:intrinsic-generated-defect 2800–2826 δ_D=d_D(D) for a licensed, previously frozen typed diagnostic invariant under unmarked role isomorphisms; nonzero status is generated content, not failure DEFINITION-VERIFIED, not a universal physical defect theorem
p2:def:gp2-realization-defect 2828–2873 Five-component candidate-relative diagnostic (ins,omit,rel,type,clos); zero iff all components vanish, with candidate-generated closure authoritative DEFINITION-VERIFIED; closure and omission components may overlap
