p2:cor:no-idle-licensed-constructor (3049–3081) is not counted as a new source object: its source argument was already reviewed (Batch 37). It is rechecked and linked as an input to the present defect examination.

B39-2 — Backward proof slice, source clauses and exact quantifiers

  1. Freeze chronology and sorts: p2:def:intrinsic-generation-grammar (1416–1443) and p2a:def:frozen-constructor-origin-certificate (2197–2230) distinguish a previously licensed typed constructor from an ambient mathematical operation. The defect definition requires d_D∈Σ_R before candidate selection, and invariance under unmarked role-isomorphisms. A candidate-dependent diagnostic can be generated inside M, but may not be promoted to earlier inventory P_{<R} as its own selection mark.
  2. Diagnostic role is not a GP2-failure condition: δ_D can be the nonzero order-swap commutator, kernel, stabilizer, etc. Its target sort is part of the constructor. If it is generated and required, GP2 must preserve it. There is no general equation δ_D=0 in GP2.
  3. Candidate realization: D_R^{GP2}(M)=(D_ins,D_omit,D_rel,D_type,D_clos) is assessed against the complete typed candidate-internal generated closure Gen_R(M) and chronological prior inventory. The conditions are not five unrelated new principles. D_clos is intentionally diagnostic and may overlap D_omit and D_type.
  4. GP2 is stipulated at its actual constitutional scope: GP2_R(M) ⇔ D_R^{GP2}(M)=0 (2875–2890), expanded through four clauses (2894–2940). Pres_M(M)=Gen_R(M) at 2942–2949 is the candidate-internal exactness subclause; the no-insertion clause must still prevent promotion of candidate-private self-structure into prior comparison marks. The source explicitly excludes cross-candidate monotonicity at 2950–2960.
  5. No-idle corollary: if a licensed C already has all generated inputs and declares required nonredundant output y=C(inputs), then omission of y implies D_clos ≠0, usually also D_omit or D_type (3049–3081). It does not require outputs of unlicensed constructors, nor those whose declared prerequisites have not been generated.
  6. Failure branch: a GP1-finite, GP2-exact realization may fail to exist. The source says to report an FRC obstruction, not modify the intrinsic defect to zero or append a new repair category (3083–3095). This is a role-specific conditional, not an independent physical gap.
  7. Rank-one example: 8218–8243 consumes the already-established rank-one role/globalization proof. The nonzero commutator signature of the source-sensitive branch must be retained; the specific SU(2)→SO(3) quotient imposes a new central equality where the source-role presentation did not generate one. This relies on the source's relation-exactness semantics, not the invalid generic assertion that every quotient of any nonabelian group is forbidden.
  8. Direct import: Paper II-F technical appendix (lines 107–116) cites both definitions and correctly distinguishes a generated diagnostic residual from a GP2 failure. Its later scalar-field stationarity calculation is a separate physical-action claim, not discharged by the defect definitions alone.

B39-3 — Independent exact mathematical controls (E3)

Nonzero intrinsic commutator. On a finite complex two-dimensional carrier, choose

X=((0,1),(1,0)), Z=((1,0),(0,-1)). Then XZ−ZX=((0,−2),(2,0))≠0. If the commutator is a licensed generated diagnostic, a faithful realization must retain it; the nonzero output is not an imperfection of the realization.

Quaternion quotient stress test. In Q8, [i,j]=−1≠1. Under the central quotient Q8/{±1}≅V4, this element maps to the identity, and all quotient commutators vanish. An exact role requiring the original generated signed commutator is lost under that quotient. This is a finite analogy for the source's rank-one relation-fidelity argument; it is not a GP2-admissible countermodel to the SU(2) allocation.

Closure is least generated content. For a finite frozen grammar root → τ → β, the iterated closure of {root} is exactly {root,τ,β}. Removing β after τ has appeared violates a licensed required-output rule. Removing the τ→β schema from the frozen grammar, or withholding τ, removes this obligation. This proves the conditional no-idle inference by direct finite closure, not by physical intuition.

Candidate exactness is not cross-candidate inclusion. Two different complete presentations, e.g. native finite cyclic groups of orders 2 and 3, each may preserve its own generated multiplication and tests; Gen(M₂)⊆Pres(M₁) is not implied. This matches the source's explicit exclusion at 2950–2960. GP3's structured order is a separate comparison, not a consequence of GP2 exactness alone.

Source-issued identity quotient. If prior generated relations actually identify two labels, exactification may retain one equivalence class. If no such identification is licensed, deleting one label violates no-omission. This distinction depends on source-generated congruence, not the analyst's preference to compress.

B39-4 — Adversarial claim-matched boundaries (E4)

  • Wrong-signature attack: A quotient/compression that maps a generated nonzero commutator to zero fails D_rel; it is not licensed merely because the quotient is a mathematically familiar object. If the diagnostic is not licensed, the same raw algebraic commutator alone creates no physical GP2 requirement.
  • Wrong-sort attack: retaining a required test as an isomorphic object-slot label fails D_type; a mathematical isomorphism does not erase the typed target requirement.
  • Missing-output attack: absent output of a present, licensed, nonredundant constructor can trigger both D_clos and D_omit; the tuple does not require independent components.
  • Self-selection attack: an invariant computed only inside a proposed candidate cannot be added to the pre-candidate inventory to select that candidate. This is the explicit no-insertion chronology rule.
  • FRC attack: on a fixed one-dimensional complex algebra, every commutator vanishes. If a genuinely frozen role demands a nonzero commutator and only this carrier is allowed, no zero-realization-defect candidate exists; it is an obstruction, not a requirement to set the intrinsic defect to zero. No claim is made that the complete Born–Hopf candidate domain is restricted this way.
  • GP2 postulate status: executable checks confirm elementary consequences of the declared semantics; they do not independently prove GP2, G0 occurrence, GP3 leastness, an entire physical carrier, or a new physical observable.

Reproducible controls: Proofs/Paper2A-GP2DefectBatch39-v1_1-verify.py and corresponding results. 47/47 finite exact controls passed. The count includes finite-model checks, not independent physics experiments or a formal soundness theorem for the complete framework.

B39-5 — Exact issue/impact adjudication

New certified Paper II-A gaps: none in these five scoped source objects. An intrinsic defect being nonzero is not evidence that GP2 fails, nor is an admissible nonzero kernel a new required physical repair. The four pre-existing issues remain separate and unchanged:

  • BH-IIA-PORT-001 — missing universal full-parent affine/Lie port for the stronger all-candidate assertion. Neither defect definition supplies the port.
  • BH-IIA-QUOT-EMPTY-001 — nonempty-family quantifier omitted from convenience quotient.
  • BH-IIA-QUOT-SURV-001 — quantifier must range over GP3-surviving classes.
  • BH-IIA-MOD-GRAPH-001 — comparison-conservative readout graph certificate.

G0-FORMULATION-COMPAT-B33 remains a formulation-compatibility task, and historical BH-IIA-DAG-LINK-B35-001 remains repaired in the active linked index. Neither is reclassified as a Paper II-A mathematical gap. The previously verified primitive S¹/S³ skeleton, generated H₁, and m_p=1 remain unchanged. The rank-one worked example is only the source's application of prior global-form claims, not new evidence for the unresolved universal Lie-port sentence.

Status: PARTIALLY_AUDITED_WITH_4_INHERITED_PAPER_ISSUES. Five new claim/source-object dispositions, one prior no-idle input rechecked, 0 new gap IDs. This is not an audit of every Paper II-A definition, imported proof, or physical observable.

Batch 40 — GP2 reciprocal role, finite images and same-role distinction

B. E1 backward proof replay, types and chronology

  1. GP2 is the actual parent premise. In p2:post:GP2, lines 2986–3032, clause (b) asserts objectification only for a generated separating test spectrum with its own faithful operation role and autonomous variance; clause (c) asserts relation-free joint composition when there is no prior generated cross-relation. Line 3031–3032 specifies Cartesian product for the compact-Hausdorff parent presentation. The theorem at 3097–3158 is a scoped instantiation of these clauses, not their mathematical derivation from an unspecified group duality.
  2. Typed objectification. The B_A operation law is contravariant/opposite relative to the active parent operation. Merely giving B_A a point-separating parametrization, without generated autonomous object/role typing, does not discharge clause (ii). The theorem does not equate B_A with an independently occurring G0 root or a second copy of A in the original carrier.
  3. Joint-support proof. Clause (iii) is about a frozen relation-slot item restricting simultaneously admissible pairs. A total comparison map A→B_A or evaluation A×B_A→Q is a function/constructor between the types, not automatically a graph predicate narrowing the admissible support. GP2 no-insertion excludes an ungenerated graph condition; its explicit relation-free-composition clause selects A×B_A. The source result is a role-support product, not a statement that all possible physical preparations/events independently occur.
  4. Born exchange. Only after a licensed isomorphism β_A is generated is the map (a,b)→(β_A^{-1}(b),β_A(a)) defined on the product. Direct substitution gives its square as identity. The graph of β_A is its fixed-point subset, but is not thereby the full physical joint support. p2a:cor:born-invertible-corealization at 4482–4511 uses the same hypotheses and the canonical licensed operations; arbitrary co-realized nodes have no such involution without the bridge.
  5. Finite-image object typing. p2:lem:gp2-finite-generated-image-inheritance (3160–3194) already appeared in the Batch 33 ledger. It produces only the finite nonredundant image Z=im F in the inherited object sort, not the entire ambient Y nor all induced derivations. Rechecking its premise avoids duplicate counts.
  6. Same-role contrast. p2:lem:gp2-same-role-no-splitting (3196–3235) applies when no autonomous role mark/variance/separating test/cross-role carrier has been generated. The independent opposite-variance premise of the reciprocal theorem is precisely the type of datum that places its B_A outside the same-role lemma's antecedent. These are complementary, not conflicting, cases.
  7. FRC scope. GP1-finite realization is explicit for generated object images (3169–3171). For the reciprocal B_A, the theorem asserts the GP2 role-typing/product implication; full actual finite/bounded realization must still pass GP1. No numerical coordinate scale, position–momentum normalization, local cotangent identification, state, history or measurement is generated by this step (3135–3136).

C. E3 independent mathematical certificates

C1. Nonabelian finite opposite role

Use A=S_3 with multiplication ab and let B_A=S_3^{op}. The inversion map β(a)=a^{-1} is an isomorphism S_3→S_3^{op} because β(ab)=b^{-1}a^{-1}=β(a)*_{op}β(b). On the 36-point full product, B(a,b)=(b^{-1},a^{-1}) obeys B²=id. Exactly six pairs (a,a^{-1}) are fixed and lie on the graph, whereas the full product has 36 pairs. Hence existence of β does not entail the graph support constraint. In the regular left action on the six-point group, the induced pullback on separating delta functions satisfies P_{ab}=P_bP_a and is faithful.

This is a finite exact algebraic control, not a physical realization of the framework's particular reciprocal sphere.

C2. Finite generated object image in infinite ambient host

On the round S³(R)⊂R⁴, the gradients of the coordinate restrictions x_i are V_i=e_i-(x_i/R²)x. Their brackets are

[V_i,V_j]=(x_i e_j−x_j e_i)/R², for i<j.

These six Killing vector fields are tangent, linearly independent and invariant under the host isometries. The literal set image of the bilinear bracket β is not the full six-dimensional vector space: in \Lambda^2\mathbb R^4 it consists of decomposable bivectors and is a proper subset. The complete six-dimensional object Z_β=span_R(im β)=im(\barβ) follows only because addition/scalar multiplication are already constituted in the parent section-object target; alternatively Paper II-B gives a literal two-bracket constructor with exactly this set image (II-B 999–1056). The ambient vector-field space Γ(TS³) is infinite-dimensional (the fields x_0^n[V_0,V_1] are linearly independent for arbitrarily large n), and its full physical realization is not entailed by realizing the six-dimensional generated image. The induced derivations on smooth scalar functions are a distinct typed operation node when licensed.

The independent check tests the rank of the generated linear hull, not a false equality between im β and its span. It also tests host-type inheritance; it does not independently prove the frozen physical constructor license or give a second momentum G0 root.

C3. Independent compactness theorem

The product of two compact Hausdorff spaces is compact Hausdorff. In the finite discrete S_3 model all 36 pairs are allowed in the absence of a frozen constraint. A diagonal/graph constraint would reduce the support to six and would be a distinct presentation. This confirms the compact-Hausdorff product step, but the physical choice between those presentations comes from the declared GP2 relation slot, not topology alone.

D. E4 adversarial boundary checks

  • Graph inserted: b=β(a) produces a 6-element graph inside 36 possible pairs; it violates clause (iii) if it was not previously licensed. If it was already generated as a support relation, the theorem's antecedent fails; do not refute the theorem with that case.
  • Opposite variance absent: A renamed point parametrization of A does not acquire a separate physical reciprocal sort. The autonomous-role premise fails; the theorem abstains.
  • Same-role internal image: A canonical six-dimensional vector-field image with no generated autonomous role split remains a generated object within R. The opposite-variance reciprocal case is not captured by this no-splitting lemma.
  • Ambient operation availability: Multiplication, tensoring or differentiation of arbitrary unlicensed ambient objects does not produce new physical carrier obligations; frozen licensed F and exact native-sort output are necessary.
  • Actuality vs role support: A×B_A is the universal relation-free role-level comparison support, not a proof that arbitrary real-world physical event pairs or measured values occur.
  • Prior-versus-current chronology: A candidate-private graph or symmetry cannot be promoted to the prior frozen inventory as its own selector. GP2's candidate-internal generated closure is assessed separately.
  • Universal Lie-parent portability: A faithful finite dual of this specific generated reciprocal pair is not an all-candidate faithful affine action of every closed primitive parent. Hence the inherited BH-IIA-PORT-001 issue is neither closed nor needed for these typed consequences.

The executable verifier Proofs/Paper2A-ReciprocalRoleBatch40-v1_1-verify.py uses exact finite S_3 data and symbolic round-S³ brackets. 1,705/1,705 individual exact assertions passed in 16 labelled groups. These assertions are not independent experiments or a proof of GP2 itself.

B41.3 — Independent proof chain: what actually establishes a common speed

The source deliberately uses two different operations:

  1. Support image: if (j_Q:\operatorname{Supp}(Q)\to \mathcal N_H) is a nonzero host-equivariant map, its image is a nonzero invariant subobject of the frozen irreducible (\mathcal N_H). By the unique-irreducible-support hypothesis, (\operatorname{im}j_Q=\mathcal N_H). This establishes which host support the descendant reaches. It does not, by that argument alone, say that (j_Q) has zero kernel.
  2. Principal relation: a distinct already licensed typed constructor must establish that the descendant's order-two principal symbol is inherited from the frozen normalized host owner and that no principal-order deformation has been generated. The previously audited p2:thm:gp2-generated-owner-normalization-inheritance then preserves its relative temporal/spatial coefficient. Up to an invertible zeroth-order endomorphism (A_Q), the conditional conclusion is

[ \sigma_2(P_Q)(\tau,\xi) =(\tau^2-c_{\rm op}^2 h^{-1}(\xi,\xi))A_Q. ]

For a finite internal rank (r), (\det \sigma_2(P_Q)=q_{\rm op}^{r}\det A_Q). Since (A_Q) is invertible, the characteristic set coincides with (q_{\rm op}=0). Only on the separately assumed hyperbolic finite-propagation domain whose wavefront boundary is governed by that cone does the physical front-speed equality follow. A same-support operator (\partial_t^2-c^2\Delta_h) with (c\ne c_{\rm op}) violates principal inheritance, not the support theorem.

The later photon/scalar corollary is correctly conditional on a later physical photon or scalar channel and a certified undeformed owner-principal bridge. It does not establish measured light speed or choose units.

B41-new issue: BH-IIA-NEUTRAL-IMAGE-001

B41.4 — Independent proofs for the remaining GP2 and compact-group claims

Canonicity versus marked choice. A licensed word whose outputs are all equivalent under an already frozen equivalence identifies a single unmarked orbit even if individual outputs have automorphisms. Conversely, choosing one of two inequivalent orbits would use data beyond the licensed word. Thus the source's GP2 canonicity lemma is a conditional no-new-choice criterion, not a theorem licensing every mathematical universal construction.

Repair theorem and its order guard. If a replacement predicate is true of the same retained object it is a reclassification, not a new physical output. When a preorder has one maximal true element, that is a greatest element if every true element is bounded above by some maximal (automatically in a finite poset) or if the true set is directed. In the infinite poset (\mathbb N\sqcup{m}), with usual (\mathbb N) order and (m) incomparable to (\mathbb N), (m) is the sole maximal but not greatest: every integer has a successor and no integer is below (m). The theorem explicitly includes the necessary hypothesis and abstains otherwise. A new object from a licensed Orig_Σ word remains conditional on the original nonobstructed exact realization and typed required-output contract; uniqueness of the mathematical target is not a physical constructor.

Constitutive fidelity. Under the source's explicit exact equality (\mathcal R(F_r)=\mathcal R_{\rm gen}\iff r=r_{\max}), the exact GP2 premise eliminates all (r<r_{\max}). This is not numerical extremization by GP3. For an invertible finite matrix (F), every positive (rF) is injective, but only (r=1) equals a normalized relation fixed as (F). Therefore mere injectivity or positivity cannot produce the endpoint. The physical role may be a constitutive fidelity only if previously typed as one; a measured response amplitude is excluded by the theorem.

Exact host closure. At nonredundant generated typed presentation-item level, GP2 no-insertion gives (\operatorname{Pres}^{\rm gen}\subseteq\operatorname{Adm}), and no-omission plus host-faithfulness gives the reverse. This equality is a statement about generated items/types, not arbitrary multiplicity, concrete operator coefficients or physically occurring state members. If the exact generated closure cannot be GP1-realized, the actual exact candidate family is empty and FRC-obstructed. GP3 comparison occurs after this stage and does not minimize unmarked coefficients.

Compact group connectedness. For compact connected Hausdorff (G), the point-separating family of compact connected Lie quotients (G_i) and the maximal-torus surjectivity of the power map supply nonempty compact root preimages. Directed finite intersections and compactness provide an (n)-th root in (G). Conversely, the profinite quotient (G/G_0) inherits divisibility; every finite quotient of a divisible group is divisible, while no nontrivial finite group is divisible (choose (n=|F|)); hence (G/G_0) is trivial. This proves the printed equivalence without assuming all full parents are Lie. It does not itself license a physically occurring connected transfer root. The same-stage requirement is precisely what excludes a post-hoc root imported from another generated group.

4. E3 — independent mathematical replay

4.1 Rooted structural equivalence

Represent a finite chronological generated presentation by a typed directed multigraph of root nodes, constructor ports, output nodes, and declared incidences. Let ≡_Σ be the congruence generated by the frozen word's licensed identities. In a GP2-exact realized presentation, every generated node has an image (no omission), every realized structural node has a generated preimage (no insertion, with all independent roots in the root tuple), and two source classes collapse only if ≡_Σ already equates them (typed host/identity faithfulness). These are surjectivity, totality, and injectivity on the quotient. A finite structure-preserving bijection with preserved port incidence and operations is an isomorphism of the specified typed presentation sort. This proof is conditional on the source's exact RealStr domain; it does not claim a bijection between all mathematical derived theorems and physically occurring tokens.

Under an unresolved GP3 family, the family as a single generated role node and its common subpresentation may be carried through this isomorphism. A particular member remains unselected, consistent with the already integrated common-value/survivor repairs.

4.2 Incidence bridges and AND versus OR

For licensed constructor w:(a,b)→x, its typed incidence graph contains a→w→x even if b is absent as an independently considered root. The arrow does not license x; the physical constructor is available exactly when both inputs and its frozen C(w) have been certified. In a fixed finite DAG grammar, define S_{n+1}=S_n∪{o(w):C(w),I(w)⊆S_n}. By induction on the first insertion stage, every newly added node admits a finite rooted AND-tree. Conversely, induction on tree height places every internal output in some S_n. For alternatives w1 or w2 with the same output, a single complete AND-tree suffices, but the existence of one route does not select a GP3 member.

A root r is indispensable for output x within that frozen word grammar precisely when x is derivable from R but not from R\{r}. A drawn path from r to x is neither sufficient for generated output nor proof of root indispensability. The theorem expressly freezes the constructor and C predicates across the deletion test; if one changes the actual physical grammar, it is a different comparison.

4.3 Neutral ancestry and retained propagation

For an equivariant j:V→N with irreducible nonzero image N, im j=N does not imply ker j=0 (e.g. j:C²→C, (a,b)↦a). The patched corollary's Supp_H(Q) names the host-visible image and separately guards full-source injectivity. No new kinetic normalization is inferred: inherited front speed still consumes a licensed same-owner undeformed principal operator relation and hyperbolic wavefront-domain premise.

4.4 Lie-port repair exact domain

From any compact connected noncommutative H, a commutator-separating Peter–Weyl representation has a compact connected Lie image K, with nonzero semisimple Lie algebra. The witness image may not be relation-exact. The existing GP3 definition admits its retained germ only after E_adm, whose nonempty compact exact class can be witnessed by T^r×S, and whose members retain the simply connected semisimple source subgroup S. Each contains an embedded simple-root SU(2) and admits a complete endpoint. This witnesses the candidatewise GP3 route independently of a faithful finite representation of H itself. For a rich Geo_R with extra constituted directions or tests, the endpoint comparison remains conditional on preserving those diagrams. This proof does not produce a universal Lie-port theorem or an empirical transfer token.

3. E1 backward dependencies and claimed observables

Born covariance route (AND): p2:post:GP2 exact typed retention (about 2875–2948) + the frozen origin contract p2a:def:frozen-constructor-origin-certificate (2206–2239) + the no-idle generated role theorem (2241–2287) + a licensed full reciprocal role and comparison isomorphism p2:thm:gp2-generated-reciprocal-role-closure (3106–3167; Batch 40) + the same-owner unmarked theorem (2352–2430) → generated Born inheritance (4552–4607). A nontrivial target action yields covariance, not the equality of numeric values. If Born moves a retained mark, there is a distinct OR route through the separately certified transported word and commuting squares (4639–4682).

Born parity subroute (AND): generated inherited equivariance + complete Born-fixed input BD=D + a target linear involution imply q=B_Q q and q_-=(I-B_Q)q/2=0. A surviving family as an object can be invariant without every member being fixed; the corollary explicitly avoids a GP3 member selector. No independent physical odd-sector elimination is asserted where the hypothesis of a Born-fixed input fails. No time, scale, action or state is generated by this abstract parity result.

Noether (separate mathematical route): same-owner scalar action invariance plus an actual differentiable one-parameter orbit and a valid chain-rule domain yield dS_D(R_ξ(D))=0. The finite linear generator lemma covers one admissible regularity case. Turning that into a local current, global charge or quantum Ward identity requires an already licensed variational/integration calculus, boundary assumptions and quantum measure/anomaly conditions respectively. Source 4984–5006 correctly labels these separate consumers rather than inventing Noether as GP4.

Recursive GP route: GP2 frozen provenance and generated-frontier rules (source 2206–2287, GP2 2875–2948); GP3 complete admissible candidate comparison (around 3713–3795); exact-moduli theorem (source 5096 onward, corrected in v3.207); current dependency-ledger frontier completeness (4804–4913); theorem-internal versus analytic certification (4915–4939) → relative GP application. What was missing in source was that the extended candidate class may be empty, so the positive successor presentation is not always defined. In source v3.209 the defined result is explicitly a three-way alternative: FRC obstruction, a selected least nonempty structured class, or a nonempty unresolved irreducible family. The frozen prior is not revised on obstruction. The empty-frontier fixed point is only asserted when the prior nonobstructed closure exists, which is the corollary's actual domain.

4. E3 independent mathematical replay and exact counter-shortcuts

  1. Born product and fixed graph. For any bijection β:A→B, the exchange B_β(a,b)=(β^{-1}(b),β(a)) squares to identity on the full Cartesian product. Its fixed-point set is exactly the graph {(a,β(a))}—a smaller set for |A|>1. GP2's relation-free product cannot be replaced by that graph unless the independent generated support relation exists. The generated isomorphism alone does not select a physical preparation.
  2. Equivariance vs invariance. On R², let the Born involution swap coordinates and define a typed two-component readout w(x,y)=(x+y,x-y). It obeys w(y,x)=diag(1,-1)w(x,y) yet is not invariant on non-fixed inputs. This makes the theorem's target-action qualifier logically necessary. If x=y, the odd output is zero. A set {+1,-1} can be stable under sign exchange while neither of its two members is fixed, preventing the invalid passage from invariant-family object to selected fixed member.
  3. Marked transport. On successive typed vector spaces V_j, invertible maps B_j and licensed constructors C_j with C_j^B B_{j-1}=B_j C_j yield C_m^B…C_1^B B_0=B_m C_m…C_1. This does not identify the original presentation with its marked reciprocal one. Missing a transported word or a commuting square prevents the inference; physical forward transport is not manufactured by a mathematical isomorphism.
  4. Dual-operation hierarchy needs stability. With X={0,1}, effects E={(0,0),(0,1)}⊂{0,1}^X, evaluation separates effects and the effect (0,1) distinguishes states. The map T swapping the two points sends that effect to (1,0)∉E: no dual E→E satisfies the evaluation equation. Thus bare separating evaluation does not imply an evaluation-dual operation; the already printed stabilizer theorem (ED-STAB) requires P_T(j(E))⊆j(E). Even when stable mathematically, a predeclared physical operation certificate is distinct, and reciprocal objectification needs its GP2 clauses. This disproves only the unguarded reading of a schematic remark and motivates its clarification; the actual guarded theorems are not refuted.
  5. Identity vs equivalence. Two abstract groups or modules may be isomorphic but have different generated owner/provenance. A generated same-sort identity congruence collapses labels; an isomorphism or numerical equality does not. The structural manifestation/deduplication corollaries are scoped precisely to the generated identity sort, not to arbitrary functorial mathematical equivalences.
  6. Empty next-stage countercontrol. Let an already nonempty finite exact candidate set be M_n={0,1}. An independently frozen later frontier adds two exact tests with admissible loci A={0}, B={1}. Then M_{n+1}=M_n∩A∩B=∅. This is a legitimate mathematical test of a licensed candidate-filter problem's FRC-obstructed possibility, not an assertion that those contradictory tests both physically occur in a completed Born–Hopf model. The former unqualified arrow P_n→P_{n+1} has no physical terminal object to denote. The source's own FRC alternative applies and leaves P_n intact. If the intersection is nonempty, relative GP comparison proceeds on that complete family; if it has multiple irreducible survivors, no arbitrary member is chosen.
  7. Empty frontier fixed point. On a nonobstructed already closed stage, adding no generated requirements changes neither its GP1/GP2 exact realization class nor its GP3 comparisons; hence no new stage information is produced, as the fixed-point corollary states. This result is independent of and compatible with the new obstructed-extension branch.

BND-B44-001 — Which observable and what sort is actually being derived?

B44.3 — Independent E3: the decisive mathematical statements

Spin(4) word length is exactly total weight

Represent irreducibles by V_(a/2,b/2) with nonnegative integers a,b. Every fundamental tensor factor changes one SU(2) highest-weight index by at most one; after r factors, a+b≤r. Conversely, a word containing exactly a left fundamentals and b right fundamentals contains the highest-spin constituent V_(a/2,b/2) by repeated SU(2) Clebsch–Gordan decomposition. Thus its minimum length is exactly r=a+b, i.e. ℓ(V_(jL,jR))=2jL+2jR. The union of all word factors up to 2m has precisely the support jL+jR≤m as the statement at 6078–6101 claims. This calculation is independent of the paper's argument and makes no statement about whether the physical grammar actually contains every word.

At the mathematical budget m=1, the possible types are (0,0), (1/2,0), (0,1/2), (1/2,1/2), (1,0), (0,1) with dimensions 1+2+2+4+3+3=15. This matches, but does not generate, the established initial H1. The identity must not be inverted to make the optional tensor grammar the constitutional origin of m_p=1.

The bracket image is not its linear hull

On S³(R) let f_u(x)=u·x; then ∇f_u=u−(u·x)x/R² and, on the sphere, [∇f_u,∇f_v]=(f_u v−f_v u)/R². These fields are infinitesimal ambient rotations. The linearized map Λ²R⁴→so(4) is an isomorphism and therefore the real linear hull of the bracket outputs has dimension six. But a single decomposable u∧v has skew rank at most two, while the perfectly valid rotation e₁∧e₂+e₃∧e₄ has skew rank four and is not one bracket. II-B expressly distinguishes these claims, and the section-object target has already constituted addition. The actual physical import is precisely that licensed target-vector-span output, not an arbitrary higher tensor constructor.

Static finite algebra cannot recover a parent generator

For finite Hermitian H and an injective relabeling f of its spectrum, Lagrange interpolation provides g with g(f(H))=H, so C*(H)=C*(f(H)) although the numerical eigenvalues can differ. If f is not injective, distinct spectral projections can merge. Thus an algebra-only input cannot identify which parent differential-order/dispersion/symbol is meant. A generated parent filtration and compression map are extra declared data, not deducible from the algebra.

Finite versus asymptotic targets

An error bound r_B(m)→0 does not assert eventual exact vanishing; for instance r_B(m)=1/(m+1) is positive at every finite level. Conversely a genuine fixed-band exact identity implies zero error on that band beyond a stated threshold. The admitted status must be read from the actual theorem and remainder, not from the adjective “large”. A sequence of finite algebraic frontiers can have unbounded least harmonic resolution across its length without realizing an infinite-dimensional frontier at any individual stage.

It independently constructs the left/right SU(2) Clebsch–Gordan successor graph through word length 10; checks support inclusion, parity, minimum length and counts; verifies the six types and 15-dimensional mathematical budget at m=1; computes the round S³ gradient brackets symbolically; checks rank of six bivectors and nondecomposable rank-four rotation; interpolates invertible and noninvertible finite spectral relabelings; tests AND-constructor closure and unlicensed square generation; enumerates 64 simultaneous two-test filters, including empty FRC cases; and compares controlled asymptotic with exact finite equality.

The principal negative controls are not complete GP2 physical countermodels: an unlicensed mathematical square exists but cannot be added to a frozen constructor output; a single bracket need not generate the entire linear span as a set, yet target addition does; a finite algebra's generator has many injective relabelings; and an empty later complete GP1/GP2 filter cannot create an output token. These all validate restrictions already present in the source, not source failures.

45.3 — E3 independent mathematical proofs and boundary attacks

Connectedness without an ambient Lie port

For connected C⊂K containing identity e, its finite product C^n is connected because multiplication is continuous and C^n is the continuous image of the connected product space C×…×C. Each C^n contains e and is contained in C^(n+1), so the union is connected, and so is its closure. If this closure is the compact topological parent, it is connected. The argument requires no finite matrix representation. Conversely, connectedness would not follow from a separate discrete seed; absence of that seed is part of the stated primitive input. A nontrivial effective action supplies K≠{e}. The Lie label is not inferred.

Total boundedness is stronger than finite diameter and weaker than compact X

For any nonempty metric space X, total boundedness gives a finite 1-net and hence finite diameter by the triangle inequality. The converse fails: the countably infinite discrete metric d(i,j)=1 for distinct integers has diameter 1 but arbitrarily large 1/2-separated subsets, so it is not totally bounded. Conversely (0,1)∩Q is totally bounded but noncomplete and noncompact; its canonical completion is [0,1]. A compact interval has distances accumulating at zero and no positive minimum separation. Therefore source claims N_X(eps)<∞, compact Xhat, and not compact/finite X follow exactly as printed.

Rate family and synchronized composition

If all v(Phi) are finite and V_prop is totally bounded in the ordinary real metric, then sup V_prop=c_op<∞; each pairwise ratio is bounded by v(Phi) and hence by c_op. With jointly continuous metric d, limits of common-bounded trajectories inherit the inequality; for finitely many matched elapsed junctions, triangle inequalities add intervals so the same c_op remains sufficient. A locally finite collection of junctions intersects any compact elapsed interval finitely. If a flow satisfies Phi_(t+s)=Phi_t Phi_s over all real t, then (Phi_t)^n=Phi_(nt). Substitution u=nt,w=ns gives exactly v((Phi_t)^n)=n v(Phi); hence a nonstationary full synchronized family cannot have bounded rate image. This says nothing about an unlicensed operation or about a constructor accounting n elapsed durations rather than one: those are different timed signatures.

On S¹ with geodesic arc metric and ordinary time, Phi_t(z)=e^{iωt}z has v=|ω|; its spatial group is compact for any ω, while the rate family for ω=n is unbounded. Thus compactness of the group does not prove the finite timed ceiling. For V_prop={1-1/n:n≥1}, the closure is {1-1/n:n≥1} ∪ {1}; the supremum 1 exists as the closure maximum yet is not attained by any member. This exactly validates the source's closure-versus-attainment qualifier.

Skeleton projection is not an additional selection proof

The source's S_1 \preceq_R^{sk} S_2 notation is defined by the existence of compared complete lifts. In general mathematical preorders, existential projection onto equivalence classes with more than one lift does not itself guarantee transitivity unless comparison respects the projection (the source does not use it to select a particular lift). For example, abstract full candidates a,b₁,b₂,c with the only nontrivial comparisons a≤b₁ and b₂≤c, and with b₁,b₂ projecting to the same skeleton, yield projected A≤B and B≤C but not A≤C. This abstract toy does not establish such a failure inside Adm_R, whose primitive comparison is explicitly built from skeleton chains. The full candidatewise GP3 preorder and the separately proved candidatewise endpoint reachability remain the controlling statements. Audit guard: do not use mere existence of different intermediary lifts to infer transitivity/unique carrier selection; when actually needed, check that the chain admits a complete compatible lift at the intermediate skeleton. No new certified source gap, physical countermodel or theorem failure follows from this general control.

Chronological endpoint examples

An unmarked scalar role might admit a complete U1 reduction of an internally exact T² candidate, but a role whose independently prior Geo_R requires two toral directions does not. Likewise a primitive order-sensitive role may admit a complete root-SU2 endpoint below a valid SU3 candidate, but an SU3-specific constituted bracket/test forbids that comparison. The SO3 comparison differs: source E_adm(su2) uses GP2 prior-relation exactness to prohibit an added central quotient when the compact SU2 source integration is available. It does not ban every SO3 instance in an unrelated signature. These cases validate the source's explicit chronological quantifiers instead of introducing a dimension minimization.

46.2 — Independent mathematics (E3)

FD/LR logical separation. The topological group G under GP1-TB is compact. LR gives local rectifiable connectivity and in particular local path connectedness; with FD the compact finite-dimensional connected-component structure falls under the locally compact group Lie criterion. In a connected compact Lie group a translation-invariant intrinsic length comparison with finite first-order rates can be modeled by a Carnot–Carathéodory horizontal distribution. Bi-invariance makes the horizontal space an Ad-invariant ideal; bracket-generating forces it to be all of Lie(G^0), so the homogeneous rate is a full invariant norm, not merely a singular sub-Finsler distribution. This explains why both optional hypotheses matter in the imported II-F argument; the source proves quantitative local comparison under its particular LR bounds. This independent explanation is not a substitute for its quantified hypotheses.

John ellipsoid is geometric, not a scale postulate. For a finite-dimensional norm F, the symmetric convex unit ball is bounded with nonempty interior. John's theorem gives one maximal-volume inscribed ellipsoid. Every F-isometry maps it to another maximal-volume inscribed ellipsoid, hence fixes it by uniqueness; therefore Ad(G) invariance of F implies Ad(G) invariance of the associated positive quadratic form. The abstract ellipsoid comparison does not choose a physical unit, an independent radius, or an observable c.

GP1 scale and indexed generation depth. Total boundedness satisfies N_(X,cd)(ε)=N_(X,d)(ε/c) for c>0. It carries no preferred positive length scale; rescaling even changes distances and rates without violating boundedness. Source p2:def:generated-boundary-hypothesis separately registers P_gbd(q,N0), which is a particular fixed index of constituted grammar length. For any nested sublevels Γ^(≤N), P_gbd(q,N0) implies ∃N covering support, but the converse fails for the given N0; it may only certify a larger bound, and no minimum is selected unless a separate argument supplies one.

Metric curry boundary. For complete (X,d), b_z(x)=d(z,x) gives ||b_x-b_y||_∞=d(x,y) by triangle inequality and evaluating at z=x. This mathematical identity does not imply the family is in the generated physical B-test grammar. Source p2:def:metric-partial-evaluation-closure explicitly imposes that distinct occurrence condition. Selecting a favorite z would import a mark not present in the unmarked grammar.

A1/local versus global. The Lie algebra of a connected noncommutative compact Lie image has nonzero compact semisimple derived algebra and a root su(2). The bracket of independent vectors in su(2)≅R^3 leaves their two-plane, so a noncommutative proper two-dimensional subalgebra cannot exist. The homomorphism SU(2)→L has a closed connected image but need not be injective; the source separately exactifies the compact globalization E_adm(su2)=[SU(2)] by GP2 in its unmarked primitive relation role. Thus neither an arbitrary image nor raw dimension alone asserts a complete GP3 endpoint.

Unmarked abelian and mixed kernel globalizations. For a≅R^k, every connected compact globalization is a/Λ for a full lattice Λ≅Z^k; all such groups are abstractly T^k, but no distinguished lattice basis remains. In mixed R×SU(2), Γ_0={(n,(-1)^{εn}):n∈Z} for ε∈{0,1} gives compact groups with trivial pure semisimple kernel; their center profiles differ (product group U(1)×SU(2) versus U(2)), showing the pure-kernel ban does not force unique compact globalization. The source's GP2 proof instead removes extra pure kernel via a relation-exact completed lift. Nontrivial pure semisimple quotient SU(2)→SO(3) is a mathematical possibility in other signatures, not an unconditional physical GP2 permission or prohibition.

Endpoint consequence. The previously repaired candidatewise reachability theorem compares each complete primitive candidate to an exact U(1) or SU(2) target with strictness if its skeleton differs. Therefore an irreducible GP3 candidate cannot have another skeleton; this does not prohibit multiple nonisomorphic structured lifts with the same skeleton. The derivation does not revisit or weaken intrinsic H_1, dimension 15, or the separately proved momentum m_p=1.

Old printed proof defect: The old v3.209 corollary said P_gbd "has only the existential form ∃N<∞" even though p2:def:generated-boundary-hypothesis defines the explicit indexed predicate P_gbd(q,N) with a separately registered particular bound. That is a non-equivalence: the fixed-depth predicate entails an existential bound; the existential does not establish the specified bound.

E4 exact countercontrol: Suppose a finite one-generator grammar has an actual realized element first appearing at word length 3, and the tested registered boundary is N0=2. Then ∃N=3 covers the realized support, but P_gbd(q,2) is false. This is a logical countermodel for quantifier exchange, not a claim that the hypothetical grammar is a GP2-constituted physical role.

Repair: Retain the source's separately registered indexed P_gbd(q,N), derive only the weaker existential from it, explicitly refuse the converse and any GP1 automatic registration, and preserve lack of minimality. See Audits/Paper2A-Batch46Closure-v1_1.diff. All 364 Paper II-A source labels retained; no other theorem statement changed. The existing no-scale conclusion is unchanged; no downstream observable was proved to fail or fixed by numerical fitting.

Tracking: This issue is RESOLVED IN SOURCE, not a claim that every independent GP1 or generation hypothesis across the full framework has been reviewed. The current tracked Paper II-A issue ledger has zero open entries, without a full-paper certification claim.

47.1 Exact target and first backward proof slice

Primary target: p2:def:operational-rank-one-factorization (v3.210 lines 8146–8168) and the single source theorem carrying both labels p2:thm:gp2-commutator-dichotomy and p2:thm:gp2-generated-rank-one-obstruction (8170–8226).

Let H_R=ρ_A(⟨R⟩)≤Aut(B(X)), already an effective mathematical action image, with a separately constituted generated test doctrine B_R and its action ρ_R:H_R→Aut(B_R). The theorem assumes ρ_R faithful when it asserts equivalence of noncommutativity and a B-test witness; absence of this test faithfulness does not negate the algebraic commutator branch. One-dimensional operational factorization is through a connected 1D Lie operation group, not an assertion that Cartan rank is one. The theorem is an in-role obstruction, not a global new primitive-role rank selector.

Source dependency chain actually consumed:

p2:def:geometric-role-existence (6368–6418) → p2a:lem:v281-connected-generated-group (6420–6452) → p2:thm:geometric-existence-exactification (6454–~6525) for the primitive algebraic branch → p2:post:GP2 (2887–3057) for generated-sort faithfulness and B-doctrine occurrence → p2:def:metric-partial-evaluation-closure (7392–7412), only if choosing canonical profiles as physical tests → p2:thm:canonical-metric-profile-separation (7415–~7468) for mathematical profile kernel detection → p2:thm:gp2-commutator-dichotomy (8170–8226). p2:post:GP3 (3732–3814) preserves supplied role distinctions but neither creates nor witnesses a missing test. The endpoint classification p2:thm:master-compact-role-allocation is already independently established for its own primitive-role scope and is not a premise proving the commutator equivalence.

Independent proof (E3). For a group action ρ on test objects, nonabelianity is equivalent to some nonidentity commutator c=[r,s]. If ρ is injective, ρ(c)≠id, hence as distinct automorphisms ρ(c)b≠b for some b. Conversely, c*b≠b forces c≠e. If an action ι factors as σ∘φ through a connected 1D Lie group C, its Lie algebra has zero bracket and connectedness forces C to be abelian; therefore φ([r,s])=e. If ι is the displayed inclusion, that contradicts ι([r,s])≠id. To claim that a generated physical B-test witnesses the obstruction additionally requires ρ_R faithful; mathematical canonical metric profiles witness it only under the separate metric-curry license. No rank or numerical parameter is introduced.

Sharp negative control (E4). S_3 is nonabelian but its sign action on X={0,1} factors through C_2, so a nontrivial 3-cycle commutator acts trivially on every test of X. This violates ρ_R faithfulness and therefore does not refute the theorem; it proves the condition is indispensable. By contrast, the regular action on the discrete metric space X=S_3 with all profiles b_z(x)=1_{x≠z} separates the commutator. The latter is a legitimate mathematical model, not an automatically occurring physical B-doctrine.

47.3 Independent mathematical certificates and critical boundary guards

Lie globalizations. If g is a finite-dimensional real Lie algebra, its connected Lie integrations are the quotient of its simply connected integration G~ by a discrete central subgroup K. In the compact semisimple g=su(2) case, G~=SU(2) and Z(G~)={±I}, leaving only SU(2) and SO(3) as connected global forms. Both admit finite faithful representations of their own sorts; selecting the former from the unmarked source-presentation relation exactness requires GP2's actually constituted no-extra-relation rule, not abstract global-form classification. For a noncompact abelian source R^k, compact quotients by full lattices are tori and no preferred lattice coordinates arise. Mixed lattice–center compactifications are not globally excluded by the pure semisimple-kernel lemma.

Torus equivalence and local versus global. For compact connected Lie G, any abelian Lie subalgebra lies in a maximal toral Lie algebra, and maximal tori have rank rank(G). Independent commuting X,Y exist iff rank G≥2. A two-torus subgroup supplies such a pair by differentiation, and a rank≥2 maximal torus includes a closed T² factor. Thus the three conditions in p2:thm:role-local-torus-obstruction are equivalent for Lie-certified parents. The source's H_R at this point denotes the post-GP3 endpoint skeleton (defined as such in the surrounding primitive allocation); that is known to be Lie. The same formula applied blindly to an arbitrary earlier compact connected non-Lie parent would be ill-typed. This is a necessary domain annotation, not a certified error in the selected-endpoint theorem. E4 controls use the cross-product identification su(2)≅R³, a rank-two diagonal Cartan of su(3), and product-circle rank addition.

Group characters and cocharacters. A continuous SU(2)→U(1) homomorphism differentiates to a Lie-algebra homomorphism su(2)→R; its differential vanishes because [su(2),su(2)]=su(2), and connectedness forces the whole group map to be trivial. For the reverse map, an abelian compact connected image lies in a maximal torus of SU(2); characters of U(1) have integral exponent, so z↦diag(z^n,z^{-n}). The center is seen by the parity character (-1)^{2j} on the spin j irreducible. Every faithful representation of SU(2) must include at least one center-odd summand; this is a diagnostic after global-form selection, not an independent GP3 center-bit input.

GP3 existence and no-selector limits. The present 14-object slice does not establish a global well-foundedness theorem for every hypothetical richer-role preorder. In a generic abstract preorder, neither a unique least class nor an irreducible member need exist; using such an abstract order as a supposed physical GP3 counterexample would not meet the protocol's actual typed in-domain countermodel requirement. Consequently the filter-order proposition is certified as an order-of-operations rule and the primitive selected branch as an endpoint theorem, not as a universal existence proof for all stronger role grammars. This does not reopen a primitive endpoint or the Paper II-B m_p=1 theorem.

BND47: [CONST] primitive intrinsic order-swap geometry; [REAL] candidatewise exact algebraic operation group; [GEN+REAL] generated B-test doctrine only when constituted; [CLASS] abelian/noncommutative branch; [MIN] completed GP3 role-faithful comparisons; [HOST] distance-profile family mathematically separate before metric-curry; [DER] rank-one Cartan and torus exclusion from the selected Lie skeleton; [ID/CAL] no automatic scalar clock unit, orientation, Hopf bridge, momentum calibration or level. All physical test claims retain their premise edges.

The script freezes the Paper II-A source SHA and checks rather than prints its finite assertions. It constructs explicit group laws for C₂,C₃,C₄,C₅,C₆,C₈,D₆,D₈,D₁₀,D₁₂,S₃,S₄,Q₈, checks identities, inverses, closure and complete associativity, computes all commutators and faithful metric-profile witnesses, and contrasts the nonfaithful sign action of S₃. It checks cyclic-root restrictions of cocharacters for integer exponents −12..12, Weyl sign invariance and kernel-size gcd, rational-power circle surjectivity witnesses, the exact su(2) cross-bracket and orthogonality on a finite lattice, a rank-two diagonal su(3) control, and central parity on finite SU(2) representation sums.

Total: 66,977/66,977 PASS. This is an exact finite control, not a standalone proof of the Peter–Weyl theorem, Lie integration classification, GP2 origin, unique GP3 least structured class or empirical physical B-test occurrence. E1/E3 arguments above provide the general mathematical statements conditional on their actual source hypotheses. Negative controls outside the generated physical grammar are flagged as mathematical boundary attacks, not countermodels to a constituted primitive role.

48.3 Independent mathematical proof replay (E1 + E3)

48.3.1 Scaled quaternion group and the source Hopf circle

For any radius R>0, regard a point of the round quaternion sphere as q=Ru with |u|=1. The operation q⊙r=R^{-1}qr is transported multiplication: Ru⊙Rv=Ruv, so its identity is R, and q⊙\bar q=R. It yields a genuine SU(2) regular parent without claiming that a generic pre-GP3 candidate state manifold equals this parent.

Paper I-A fixes the common-phase Hopf flow X_H(q)=iq and τ_H^+=2π X_H with period 2π; on the identity-through Hopf fiber the subgroup is e^{it}. In the same frozen quaternion model it is a smooth embedded circle of SU(2) with no newly chosen torus axis. The inclusion ι_H is injective; for u,v in that circle, uv=vu, whence ι_H^op(uv)=uv=vu=ι_H^op(u)⋆_opι_H^op(v). On the abelian subalgebra, the opposite-group bracket restriction is the same zero bracket, and exp_op(tX_H)=exp(tX_H). Thus the identical point-map inclusion into the opposite group keeps the primitive parameter.

Critical type distinction (adversarial E4): Paper I-A's opposite marked Hopf copy uses the different map q↦q^{-1} and transports X_H(q)=iq to X_H^opp(q)=-qi in its marked model. It changes the parameter e^{it}↦e^{-it}. The II-A identity-on-selected-circle ι_H^op is not that inversion map. Comparing them without recording their map and source/target sorts would falsely manufacture a handedness contradiction. Paper III-A p3a:def:clocked-hopf-propagation-doctrine explicitly uses ι_H^op and the contragredient R_{g^{-1}} convention so the inherited primitive has the intended sign.

48.3.2 Why the scalar sign is conditional but unique given provenance

A continuous circle homomorphism is conjugate (after choosing circle coordinates for a proof) to z↦z^n for integer n; continuity and a group law force the integer winding. Its kernel is the roots of unity of order |n| if n≠0; an injective homomorphism has n=+1 or −1, hence is bijective and a Lie-group isomorphism. On the lattice it acts by the unit ±1, sending a primitive to a primitive. In II-A κ_t is already supplied as an occurring frozen morphism, so e_t=(κ_t)_*τ_H^+ has an unambiguous image and sign relative to that morphism. Replacing κ_t by I∘κ_t is an alteration of the frozen data, not a symmetry of the same arrow-labelled presentation.

Without HS_t, the target scalar group remains unmarked: the two choices e_t,−e_t and their duals e_E,−e_E give identical integral pairing and dE∧dt. This is the explicit Paper II-F inversion countermodel. Neither bare U(1), bare SU(2), the no-character lemma, nor the cocharacter classification supplies the physical Hopf→succession arrow. Nothing here derives seconds, energy calibration, actual evolution, or a physical Hamiltonian. The direct III-A p3:thm:transported-scalar-orientation imports exactly this κ_t plus the positive Reeb lattice, with calibration kept separate.

48.3.3 Stagewise scalar weights and the generated-readout question

For a continuous finite-dimensional unitary U(1) action, orthogonal eigenspace decomposition gives ρ(z)=Σ_{n∈F}z^nP_n. Differentiating in the dimensionless angle gives the self-adjoint integer-weight generator Q=Σ nP_n; evaluating its own minimal-root product on each eigenspace proves ∏_{n∈F}(Q−nI)=0. The kernel of the whole action is {z:z^n=1 for all n∈F}, equivalently {z:z^g=1} for g=gcd{|n|:n≠0}. If the action is faithful then g=1, and in particular there must be some nonzero weight; it need not have weight ±1: support {2,3} is faithful. N_s=max|n| is finite for that realization only and cannot be frozen by GP1 across inequivalent possible representations.

For a frozen comparison, let D be the actual difference of the compared objects and ℓ_1,…,ℓ_k precisely the licensed consumed tests. Claim-relative agreement means every ℓ_j(D)=0 by subtraction and requires no other invisible operator equality. Full zero D=0 follows if and only if X↦(ℓ_j(X)) has trivial kernel on the actually occurring difference space. II-C proves the equivalence using a positive frame operator R^*R; this is a proof of the test-separation criterion, not a licensing of a new apparatus. With diagonal two-outcome effects on a two-level carrier, an offdiagonal traceless Hermitian difference has all measured contractions zero while remaining nonzero. It refutes the stronger unguarded inference, not II-A's conditional corollary. II-B supplies source word-defect formulas; II-D separates multiplication and reconstruction errors for actual licensed comparison maps. Neither imports an unperformed measurement.

48.3.4 Atlas types and chronological status

GEN records a role whose complete output was generated/co-realized, DL records discharge only at a later stage, ROOT records an actual G0 physical assertion with no earlier proof, and OBS records empty/incompatible branches; these tags do not constitute new objects. In particular, a classification of abstract groups in Abs(Γ) is not an embedding in GrpSupp(Γ) and neither is a physical support GeoSupp(Γ). An exact Cmp_m presentation cannot by equality of cardinalities become a microscopic position carrier. Later physical discharge may update later claim status while the earlier branch proof remains conditional. This maintains the recursive-GP/FRC branch repaired in Batch 43.

  • all 2^11−1=2047 nonempty weight supports inside [−5,5], cyclic-restriction kernels and exact weight annihilators; faithful {2,3} and nonfaithful {2,4} examples;

These tests verify concrete algebraic boundary cases and detect sign/type confusion; they do not by themselves prove the global classification of continuous circle maps, mathematical existence of the physical provenance arrow, the source–object GP2 co-realization, or all cited Paper I–VII results. 49.3 Independent proof replay of the imported exceptional group classification

Paper I-B p1b:thm:v233-subgroup-sampler-classification (its current source lines 4820–4889) directly proves the finite-subgroup classification with nonnegative normalized weights, not a secretly assumed uniform instrument. Left-averaging a mathematically exact weighted rule over its own subgroup produces a uniform exact rule for existence, but does not erase actual frozen physical weights. A cyclic group lies in a circle-plane and misses a linear test. A binary dihedral group has an intrinsic unoriented axis u; its degree-two conjugation-axis harmonic h_u has squared value one on all of its nodes, but Haar second moment 1/3, disproving complete P2 pairing even with nonuniform nonnegative weights. Hence only 2T, 2O, 2I survive. Subgroup inclusions 2T⊂2O,2I make 2T least only when frozen role comparison allows restriction of all retained data. Paper I-B p1b:prop:v233-sampling-restriction (5225–5248) proves image-unitary transport; it explicitly warns that full-node tests and whole left-regular actions may fail to transport. The source II-A roles exclude those data or require their retention in the comparison. p1b:prop:v233-hopf72 provides the exact non-group 72-node counterexample, showing why design exactness or antipodality alone never creates the finite subgroup.

Which observable is consumed: P2 Hermitian Gram and Dirichlet pairing require polynomial degree four; all P2 cubic product moments or all P2 M_f P2 entries require degree six; all real quartic scalar actions on P2 require degree eight by polarization. The exact design strengths of the exceptional groups are 5, 7, 11. Thus the source's three candidate rows follow. The displayed 2T degree-six discrepancy is 1/12−1/16=1/48. For u=q_0²−q_1², the exact quartic moments are τ_{2T}(u⁴)=1/6, τ_{2O}(u⁴)=5/48 and τ_{2I}(u⁴)=σ(u⁴)=3/40. 2O and 2I have respective order 48 and 120, neither divides the other; a cardinality comparator would be a different licensed order from subgroup inclusion. Paper II-F p2c:thm:v268-endpoint-quadratic-iff-design4 (6508–6549) supplies the actual endpoint-POVM degree-four converse, including its crucial nonzero vector-block coefficient a−(4−a)/3>0; the theorem has not been replaced by the claim that every arbitrary POVM does so.

49.4 Independent E3: algebra, representation and weights

Recentred 24-cell: With Z=aHb^{-1} and z=ahb^{-1}, z^{-1}Z=bh^{-1}Hb^{-1}=bHb^{-1}. Since h^{-1}H=H, every choice of z gives the same embedded subgroup for a fixed presentation and hence the same conjugacy orbit under different lifts. The derivation uses an existing multiplication owner and does not pick an embedded representative physically.

Full averaging: A_μ²=A_μ on all C(G) implies μ*μ=μ because evaluation at e against all inverted continuous functions separates measures. For finite strictly positive weights this forces SS=S, since supp(μ*μ)=SS. Every finite nonempty subsemigroup of a group is a subgroup: powers repeat, producing identity and inverse. On this finite subgroup let a=max_s w_s=w_{s0}. Idempotence gives a=∑_g w_g w_{g^{-1}s0}≤a∑_g w_g=a; all w_g>0 forces all weights equal to a=1/|S|. Conversely normalized subgroup Haar is idempotent.

Finite-band countermodel: On P5, μ_{g2T} and Haar have the same convolution operator because Sym^k(C²) has no 2T invariants for 1≤k≤5; the restriction is the identity on constants and zero on every nonconstant harmonic. For g∉2T, the support g2T does not contain the identity and hence cannot be a subgroup. This is not a countermodel to full-domain averaging repeatability.

First leaked degree: For k_Γ=6,8,12, the character sums |Γ|^{-1}∑_{γ∈Γ}U_k(Re γ) certify a single Γ-fixed line Cv in Sym^{k_Γ}(C²) and no positive lower-degree invariant. With P=|v⟩⟨v| and T=ρ_k(g)P, multiplication gives T²−T=(⟨v,ρ_k(g)v⟩−1)T. Its vanishing is equivalent, by unitarity, to ρ_k(g)v=v; the stabilizer is exactly Γ by the compact subgroup classification and exclusion of higher finite groups possessing an invariant in that same degree. This is a specified uniform-coset test, not an inference that an unregistered μ is a coset.

Two support-polynomial moment conditions: Each d_s(q)=1−⟨q,s⟩≥0 vanishes exactly at s on S³. Hence p_S=∏_s d_s has zero set S, and strictly positive convolution weights make ∫p_S d(μ*μ)=0 iff SS⊆S. Under subgroup closure, the nonnegative Lagrange polynomials ℓ_s have ℓ_s(t)=δ_{st}. Therefore h_μ=∑_s w_s ℓ_s interpolates the weights; ∫h_μ dμ=||w||², ∫h_μ d(μ*μ)=⟨w,w*w⟩. The convolution norm contraction plus equality of these two inner products forces w*w=w, hence uniformity. For u_s=1/N, x=w−u, α=Nw_min, w=αu+(1−α)v, and w*x=(1−α)v*x, the defect obeys δ_h≥α||x||², exactly as printed. Likewise leakage ∫p_S d(μ*μ) bounds products leaving S by the printed finite c_S>0 when they exist. All these integral and stability statements are valid.

Unmarked sixth moment: A_6^{supp}(Γ)=||M_6(Γ)−M_6^{Haar}||², not a weighted-instrument readout. For a subgroup, pair averaging reduces it to |Γ|^{-1}∑(Re γ)^6−5/64: 2T has 3/32−5/64=1/64, while exact 7- and 11-designs 2O/2I yield 0. The theorem does not license an experimental support-moment instrument, and an arbitrary positive physical measure need not share the uniform-support moment.

Loop: coev(1)=∑_i e^i⊗e_i in H*⊗H; ev((1⊗T)coev)=∑_i e^i(Te_i)=Tr T. For T=⊕_λt_λI_{H_λ}, Tr T=∑d_λt_λ. Dividing by the identity-loop value dim H produces d_λ/dim H, with no state preparation. Adding an insertion B changes the source word and gives Tr BT.

49.5 Certified issue BH-IIA-CONV-ID-001 — orientation of identity-point readouts

  • Class: TYPE_OR_DOMAIN / OPERATIONAL_READOUT (minor-to-moderate, source-local); lifecycle: OPEN pending actual source correction, not patched by this audit.
  • First origin: p2a:thm:v274-two-scalar-repeatability-certificate, II-A v3.210 lines 9578–9580, which says the displayed integral identities are equivalently repeatability against the same two support-dependent polynomial tests at the identity. The actual left action is defined in p2a:thm:v273-positive-repeatable-convolution-group, lines 9433–9435, as A_μ f(x)=∫f(g^{-1}x)dμ(g).
  • Failed direct inference: Generally A_μ f(e)=∫f(q^{-1})dμ(q), and A_μ²f(e)=∫f(q^{-1})d(μ*μ)(q). Therefore the displayed moment tests of the unmodified functions p_S,h_μ are exactly identity-point repeatability of their inverse pullbacks p_S∘inv,h_μ∘inv, not necessarily of the functions themselves.
  • In-domain counterexample: Put a=cos(2π/5)+i sin(2π/5)∈SU(2), μ=δ_a, S={a}; the source formulas give p_S(q)=1−⟨q,a⟩, h_μ(q)=1. Then [ A_\mu p_S(e)=1-\cos(4\pi/5)=\frac{5+\sqrt5}{4} =1-\cos(6\pi/5)=A_\mu^2p_S(e), \qquad A_\mu h_\mu(e)=A_\mu^2h_\mu(e)=1. ] But S={a} is not a subgroup, because it omits e; moreover the actual first displayed test fails: [ \int p_S,d(\mu\mu)=p_S(a^2) =1-\cos(2\pi/5)=\frac{5-\sqrt5}{4}>0. ] So repeatability at the identity on the unmodified printed pair does not suffice. This does not refute the *two displayed integral tests; it refutes solely the claimed unguarded identity-point reformulation.
  • Minimal repair obligation: Revise line 9578–9580 to specify the inverse-pullback polynomials p_S∘inv and h_μ∘inv for identity-point action tests. Inversion on the quaternionic unit sphere is a real-linear orthogonal map, so polynomial degrees remain N and at most N−1, and no new physical constructor/axiom is imported. The role must still separately license the actual function-valued tests. Do not weaken the valid integral certificate.
  • Impact and bypass: The immediate same-theorem operational gloss requires this correction. p2a:thm:v275-repeatability-PN-suffices, II-A 11995–12008, already explicitly evaluates q↦p_S(q^{-1}) and the inverse-pulled ℓ_s; its route is verified unaffected. The source remark at 11985–11987 calls the support-dependent tests a cheaper alternative and remains valid when used as the displayed measure moments/inverse-pulled identity tests. No source-supported inference to unconditional 2T occurrence, m_p=1, a physical speed, or a downstream paper is affected.

49.6 Executable E4 controls and scope

11,927 / 11,927 PASS, with exact Q(√5) integer/rational moment arithmetic. The script enumerates exact even moments for the 24-, 48-, and 120-point binary polyhedral coordinates through their 5-, 7-, and 11-design strengths; the exact first leaking coordinate moments at degrees 6, 8, 12; exact character ranks of Sym^k(C²) (first nonzero rank one at k=6,8,12); degree-six and degree-eight test discriminators; and unweighted sixth-support invariants. It then exhausts 9,830 nonzero integer-weight vectors of lengths 2–8 over 0,1,2 for cyclic-group positive-measure support closure and checks the second-test stability/uniformity on 558 closed-support cases. It checks the fifth-root operational counterexample symbolically in Q(√5) and validates source anchor presence. Passing these tests does not substitute for global classification or certify a physical finite support/instrument. The counterexample is a positive discovery, not a failing verifier assertion.

50.2 Source proof slice: why complete input does not force constituent-value dependence

p2:def:finite-generated-constituent-presentation (2028–2053) specifies a previously frozen nonredundant typed constructor presentation; p2:thm:generated-constituent-corealization (2056–2087) co-realizes its constituents on an occurring owner; and p2:cor:no-idle-licensed-constructor (3060–3080) retains required complete outputs whose inputs were actually generated. The verified Batch-20 p2a:thm:v3101-generated-coconsumption (9732–9765) applies exactly this chain. It does not assert that every output value changes every later scalar readout.

The other already verified Batch-20 proposition p2a:prop:v281-complete-input-factorization (9796–9841) proves the independent set-level criterion: for F:Q→Y, there exists exactly one function Ftilde:π_S(Q)→Y with F=Ftilde∘π_S iff F is constant on each fibre of π_S. This is a mathematical factorization, not a new physical compression word. The new family-readout corollary inherits the nonempty GP3 survivor guard from p2a:thm:compatible-family-observable-closure (4027–4082) and p2a:thm:generated-exact-solution-moduli (5155–5240): empty exact families are FRC-obstructed and have no common generated value; singleton image on a nonempty complete survivor family produces common content without member selection. Merely agreeing on pre-GP3 compatible candidates would be a different claim. The origin/consumer remark correctly identifies the first actual typed arrow requiring an extra physical incidence/test, rather than assigning the issue to a later paper's naming.

E3/E4: exact finite full output-tuple/fibre functions tested on all nonempty subsets of {0,1}³ up to size 4 and their binary readouts, plus the explicit empty-family boundary. No counterexample in the stated structured/typed domain. There is no claim that mathematical factors can delete frozen constructor ports.

50.3 GP1 bounded whole-region readouts; exact VI-A import

GP1 is exactly p2:post:GP1 (1473–1512): totally bounded constituted scalar/parameter/rate families in their generated uniform structure, not every ambient function family. On the new theorem's one occurring complete region and certified constructor (9854–9859), each required real family f_j(R) is itself part of the constituted physical datum. A totally bounded subset of R is bounded, giving a finite M_{R,j} for that family; attainment and an absolute stage-independent ceiling do not follow. The prior generated-role and no-idle theorems provide the co-realization step independently of the scalar bound.

With a separately proved same-owner relation f(s)≥C y(s)^{-p}>0, f(s)≤M_R yields y(s)^p≥C/M_R and the source's positive lower bound on y. The opposite inference, that GP1 produces either the relation or a numerical value of M_R, is invalid and is not asserted in source. On Paper6A-GravityCosmology-v2_250.tex, the direct consumer p8:thm:bounded-faithful-metric-realization (11409–11433) explicitly imports the bounded family only after the complete metric/action/source region and readouts exist. p8:cor:free-generator-radius-bounds (11463–11493) imports the inverse corollary only after its complete free Dirac width inequality is proved. These imported source clauses are correct in that domain, with no cross-paper unconditional physical radius or continuation re-certification.

E3/E4: 189 independent exact rational inverse-bound comparisons, plus non-attained supremum and stage-specific counter-inferences. These validate the algebra, not a universal physical width bound.

  • Paper4B-QuantumResponse-v1_39.tex, p4:thm:v2168-faithful-stationary-poles (6932–6991): an actually faithful stationary density makes a given spectral source-tag component visible precisely when its projector is nonzero. Pole locations remain common while residues depend on weights; finite Bohr poles are not automatically vacuum particle masses.

The new p2a:cor:v2169-actual-support-reuse consumes the strictly stronger exact support premise. Its equality p_{r,P}(ε_r)=0 includes visibility on that exact P; a containing support or a family with variable supports does not inherit it. This is not a proof that the fixed native terminal coefficient happens to be a physical source pole.

Independent E3: diagonal finite Hermitian H=diag(0,1,3), a three-component source q=E_{10}+E_{21}+E_{20}, with each nonempty spectral support and strictly positive weights. The normalized anticommutator source Gram gives ||E_{ij}||_ρ²=ρ_i+ρ_j; therefore for each fixed support the non-null frequency set is independent of weights. Its Hankel moment matrix is positive definite on the supported distinct frequencies, and its first annihilating polynomial is the exact product ∏(z−ω). Tested 63 weight/support realizations, reconstructing exact coefficients and testing candidate-root equivalence. Different supports give different root sets, as the theorem properly warns. This is a finite illustrative independent proof control, not a claimed physical QCD/particle realization.

50.5 Independent proof of the support-cardinality P_N certificate

For positive μ=Σ_{s∈S} w_sδ_s, |S|=N, the scalar harmonic band P_N contains the inverse pullback of

p_S(q)=∏_{s∈S}(1−⟨q,s⟩) (degree N, nonnegative, with exact zero locus S).

Because inversion q↦q^{-1} is real orthogonal on quaternionic S³, both p_S∘inv and every inverse-pulled interpolation polynomial remain in the same band. The source operator convention A_μ f(x)=∫ f(g^{-1}x)dμ(g) then gives

(A_μ²−A_μ)(p_S∘inv)(e)=∫p_Sd(μ*μ)−∫p_Sdμ = ∑_{s,t}w_sw_t p_S(st).

The sum vanishes only if SS⊆S, because every weight is positive and every factor is nonnegative. A nonempty finite multiplicatively closed subset of a group is a subgroup. On that group the degree-(N−1) Lagrange tests

ℓ_s(q)=∏_{t≠s}(1−⟨q,t⟩)/(1−⟨s,t⟩)

satisfy ℓ_s(t)=δ_{s,t}. Inverse-pulled identity-point testing forces μ*μ=μ as measures on S. The prior validated positive convolution theorem p2a:thm:v273-positive-repeatable-convolution-group (9430–9464) then implies positive uniform Haar weights. Conversely a uniform finite subgroup average is idempotent on all functions. Peter–Weyl decomposes P_N=⊕_{k=0}^N E_k, E_k carrying the left Sym^k(C²) block; thus idempotence on this band is equivalent to μhat(k)^2=μhat(k) for each k ≤ N. The sum of (k+1)||...||²_HS is basis-independent, nonnegative, and zero exactly when all blocks are idempotent.

This proof does not consume the false identity-point equivalence of the earlier v274 theorem. That theorem's moment calculations and the earlier full-positive idempotent-measure theorem remain correct, and the v275 text explicitly uses the inverse-pulled functions at 11995–12008. The existing open issue is source-local, not a blocker of this valid independent route.

E4 exact finite SU(2) torus controls: for cyclic C_m, 2≤m≤8, every positive integer-weight vector on supports of 1≤N≤5 points with coefficients in {1,2,3} was exhaustively enumerated — 34,881 cases. With denominator T=Σa_j and convolution defect D(x)=Σ[(a*a)_j−T a_j]x^j, Sym^k-band idempotence is checked exactly by reduction modulo Φ_{m/gcd(m,ℓ)}(x) for all ℓ=1,...,N. Exactly 48 of these instances meet the band criterion, and every one is precisely a uniform subgroup measure; all uniform subgroup measures found in the census pass. This is an adversarial subdomain search, not an exhaustive test of nonabelian SU(2).

Two-point sharpness

For g≠±1, let S_g={g,-g} and μ=(δ_g+δ_{-g})/2. On P₁ all nonconstant linear modes cancel (g and −g act antipodally), so the averaging is the constants projector and is idempotent, despite S omitting the identity. Degree-two p_{S_g}(q)=1−⟨q,g⟩² gives exact defect 1−(Re g)²>0. With g=i this is 1 exactly. Thus the printed degree-(N−1) insufficiency witness for N=2 is valid; it does not assert the full N bound is optimally sharp for all N.

50.6 Eighth-moment signature and coset-location distinction

Let σ be normalized Haar on S³ and M₈(ν)=∫q^{⊗8}dν(q). For a uniform subgroup Γ,

||M₈(μΓ)−M₈(σ)||²=E_{γ∈Γ}(Re γ)^8−E_{σ}(q_0)^8, with E_σ(q_0)^8=7/128.

This follows by expanding the squared tensor distance, using pair overlaps ⟨g,h⟩=Re(g⁻¹h) and subgroup averaging. The exact real-part multiplicity distributions printed at II-A 12053–12061 then give means 11/128, 15/256, 7/128 for 2T,2O,2I, respectively, hence the squared-discrepancy values 1/32, 1/256, 0. The golden-ratio identity φ⁸+φ⁻⁸=47 independently verifies the 2I term. This norm is invariant under simultaneous left rotation of the tensor and Haar, so the same values hold for the uniform left coset gΓ. They therefore cannot detect a coset's origin or prove subgroup support.

With the source's additional already-known uniform coset hypothesis, the first leaked symmetric-power degree k_Γ=6,8,12 (corresponding to the three types) has rank-one averaged invariant-line projector P=vv*. The Fourier block is T=ρ(g)P, and direct multiplication gives T²−T=(⟨v,ρ(g)v⟩−1)T, with ||T||_HS=1. By the previously verified exact invariant-vector stabilizer result p2a:thm:v274-first-leaked-harmonic-coset (9509–9555), its square defect vanishes iff g∈Γ. The two diagnostics thus separate exceptional type from coset origin, in that order. They do not prove the coset hypothesis, physical occurrence, or the presence of a corresponding instrument. Paper I-B p1b:thm:v274-branch-symmetric-twin-convolution (5072–5149) supplies the source's non-subgroup degree-11-exact measure ξ_{H,n}; its eighth-order tensor matches Haar even while its support is not a subgroup.

E3/E4: symbolic rational/Galois exact moment check, φ-identity, and exact orthogonal rank-one tensor controls. No new mathematical defect is certified.
