51.2 E3 proof: clock-normalized characteristic and action relation

Definition p2a:def:v2190-clocked-phase-word gives a real one-form ϑ_t on the already generated phase role, a real H_t, an oriented t, actual allowed variations, and nondegenerate Ω_t = −d_Pϑ_t on the retained regular chart. With α=ϑ_t−H_t dt one obtains, by the exterior differential split,

[ \beta=-d\alpha=\Omega_t+\eta_t\wedge dt, \qquad \eta_t=\partial_t\vartheta_t+d_{\mathcal P}\mathcal H_t. ]

For X=∂_t+Y_t, contraction yields

[ \iota_X\beta=(\iota_Y\Omega_t-\eta_t)+\eta_t(Y)dt. ]

Nondegeneracy gives exactly one Y_t solving ι_YΩ_t=η_t. Antisymmetry then gives η_t(Y)=Ω_t(Y,Y)=0; this proves the whole contraction equation and unique clock normalization. Conversely a clock-normalized null vector gives the same spatial equation. The first variation is δS=∫dα(V,\dotσ)+[α(V)]_{endpoints}; under the source's full vertical test and endpoint term conditions, stationarity is equivalent to ι_{\dotσ}β=0, hence to \dotσ=X. On restricted variation domains only the projected/tested Euler relation follows, exactly as the source warns.

Because d_PΩ_t=0 and ∂_tΩ_t=−d_P∂_tϑ_t, Cartan and ι_YΩ_t=∂_tϑ_t+d_PH_t imply

[ \partial_t\Omega_t+\mathcal L_{Y_t}\Omega_t=0, \qquad F_{t,s}^{*}\Omega_t=\Omega_s ]

on the common existence domain. The time-dependent energy transport law is

[ \tfrac{d}{dt}H_t(\sigma(t))=\partial_tH_t-(\partial_t\vartheta_t)(Y_t), ]

not the generally incorrect autonomous dH/dt=0. For ϑ=p_i dq^i independent of t, this reduces to the displayed Hamilton equations. On C² local coefficients with invertible Ω the field is C¹ and hence locally Lipschitz; standard local Cauchy uniqueness and compact-chart continuation follow. These are conditional mathematical representations, not constitutional selection of a physical law or state.

E4 attack: symbolic and exact rational families use ϑ_t=(a_0+a_1t)p dq and H_t=p²/(2m)+kq²/2+νt qp+λtq, so Y_q=H_p/a, Y_p=−(a_1p+H_q)/a. Exact character, energy and transport identities hold even at nonzero a_1. The excluded degenerate four-dimensional Ω explicitly has rank two and multiple characteristic directions; it violates the theorem's nondegeneracy premise and therefore is not an in-domain refutation.

51.3 Complete-owner isotropy: proved sufficiency, not symmetry by coordinates

For the actual isotropy group G_m, theorem p2a:thm:v2190-clock-isotropy-rigidity requires both β,dt preservation and equivariance of the separately licensed X_op. The complete pre-generator signature includes the action's source, boundary, field and other retained marks; it is the same-owner rule of p2a:thm:v368-same-owner-unmarked-equivariance (2354–2405). Unique clock-normalized characteristic X is fixed by every such isotropy automorphism. Therefore X_op−X∈V_m^{G_m}. If the fixed space vanishes, equality follows. The affine set X+V_m^{G_m} classifies invariant tangent directions, not independently generated operations.

In a bare presymplectic chart with transverse symplectic matrix Ω, let η=−ΩY and

[ \beta=\begin{pmatrix}\Omega&\eta\-\eta^{T}&0\end{pmatrix}, \quad X=\begin{pmatrix}Y\1\end{pmatrix}, \quad g=\begin{pmatrix}-I&2Y\0&1\end{pmatrix}. ]

Then βX=0, g^Tβg=β, dt\,g=dt, and gX=X exactly. This shows the sufficient −I transverse symmetry for the bare comparison tensors. The source itself and Paper II-F p2a:app:v2190-exactness-stationarity (108–156) and p2a:prop:v2190-retained-mark-fixed-vector (158–174) immediately prevent importing that bare transformation into a marked owner without checking its marks. If every actual isotropy fixes a nonzero ℓ∈V*, its Ω-dual Z is a nonzero fixed vector, so V^G≠0 and the zero-fixed-space premise fails. In particular the differential of a retained nonconstant Hamiltonian can obstruct bare inversion. A shear [[1,s],[0,1]] preserves both dq∧dp and dp, and fixes ∂_q; this is a controlled boundary example.

Verdict: No overclaim of automatic same-owner isotropy was found. The Paper II-F diagnostic explicitly states that symmetry invariance of S along symmetry directions is weaker than stationarity along all allowed variations. X_op=∂_t and H=p, ϑ=p dq yield a nonzero residual and demonstrate why operation provenance alone cannot replace the identification certificate.

51.4 Finite residual test, generated evolution and direct consumers

On a regular phase role and for independently typed X_op with dt(X_op)=1, \mathfrak r_{op}=ι_{X_op}(-dα) vanishes exactly when X_op=X, by uniqueness above. A covector vanishes precisely when all its coordinates in a finite actual tangent basis vanish. This is a finite certificate, not a new test or law unless the corresponding readout is licensed. The Cauchy graph and stationary relation were already required outputs of the complete word; re-presenting them by the same generated-image route does not imply a global continuation, a physical history token, or a specific initial state.

Direct downstream proof-use witnesses were inspected in the unchanged framework archive:

  • Paper III-F, Paper3F-CompleteDynamics-v1_253.tex 17703–17764: a regular already retained complete source/Legendre action yields the characteristic and complete Cauchy equations and invokes p2a:thm:v2190-clocked-characteristic. Lines 17823–17832 explicitly keep the separately licensed clock operation comparison conditional.
  • Paper IV-B, Paper4B-QuantumResponse-v1_39.tex 2595–2615: finite Hermitian Schrödinger propagation and its Volterra uniqueness are proved first; the II-A theorem supplies same-root closure after the evolution target and word are licensed, not a free dynamics postulate.
  • Paper II-F, 108–182: the retained-mark obstruction and nonstationarity of arbitrary action members are an exact limitation of the positive theorem, not contradictory theorem premises.

No global physical or interacting-continuation audit of III-F/IV-B was performed; the checked references are exact local imported interfaces.

51.5 Complete charged-packet origin and common readout

The assembly proposition's consumed premises are already established in p2a:thm:v187-pcad (5751 onward), p2a:thm:v3101-generated-coconsumption (9732–9765), and p2a:rem:constructor-origin-and-consumer (9774–9793). A diagram of existing maps can transport a certified q_r, but does not establish the independent antecedent arrow a_r that generates it. For an assembly \mathfrak A(Q) consuming (q_r)_r, all required input arrows must have a licensed result-independent origin before invoking GP2 no-idle. The proposition claims no new G0 token, because this is an input certificate on an already occurring parent; it does not replace that certificate with a printed formula.

For a nonempty GP3-retained complete family Q and a previously licensed readout F:Q→Y, if F(Q)={y_*}, the readout has the common value y_* without selecting q∈Q. Empty Q is an FRC obstruction, not a source of a vacuous physical output. If F varies, retain the complete image set; no member selector appears from GP2 or a frame rotation. This is a direct use of p2a:cor:v3101-coconsumed-family-readout and previously repaired p2a:thm:compatible-family-observable-closure, not a new unconditional row/channel prediction.

Neither uses a desired mass or pole to select a missing arrow.

51.6 Parent saturation and relation-hypergraph boundary

p2a:def:v187-parent-saturation (10549–10562) supplies the finite registry of licensed same-owner constructors Σ_P on an already constituted selected-type parent. Saturation before role-splitting is constructor-relative. It is neither closure under all mathematical maps nor a theorem that a finite registry must generate finitely many objects. The latter would fail for unrestricted iterative maps, but the manuscript does not claim it. p2a:rem:v187-relation-hypergraph (10571–10580) records the separate sorts PARENT_RELATION, DESCENT, INDUCED_ACTION, QUOTIENT, DEFECT, SAME_ACTION_RESPONSE, OCCURRENCE, ANALYTIC_CERTIFICATE, and CALIBRATION; the promotion of a relation to a binary function requires proof of functionality and the declared domain. Its previously audited positive input p2a:thm:v187-pcad explicitly distinguishes existential image descent, representative-independent saturation, functionality, totality, and action-transport conditions. Neither new item inserts a new constitutional principle or an unlicensed cross-descendant map.

52.2 Exact A1 globalization, eigenvalues, spectral observables and chronological order

At the same Lie algebra su(2), the universal connected integration is SU(2) with centre {±I}. Connected Hausdorff Lie globalizations correspond to discrete central kernels, so exactly the trivial and order-two kernel occur: SU(2) and SO(3)=SU(2)/{±I}. This is a mathematical comparison of global forms, not the candidatewise GP2 endpoint proof from earlier B42/B47.

For a round radius R, scalar harmonics of degree k on S³ have eigenvalues k(k+2)/R², multiplicity (k+1)², and antipodal parity (-1)^k. The quotient RP³ allows only even k. For k=1 on SU(2) the first nonconstant eigenspace has (λ,dim)=(3/R²,4). For k=2 on SO(3) it has (λ,dim)=(8/R²,9). Both local scalar curvatures equal 6/R², so scale-free ratios are 1/2 and 4/3. Exact rational checks at radii 1/7,1/2,1,3/2,12 confirm the scale cancellation, and 49 harmonic degrees confirm the algebraic spectrum/parity formulas.

The first scalar harmonic thus provides a testable discriminator conditional on an actual earlier licensed scalar Laplace readout in the role. Neither G0 by itself nor GP2's non-insertion rule licenses that test after inspecting an SU(2) atlas. A nontrivial compact connected circle satisfies the bare G0-level existence grammar and is abelian, so G0 alone cannot force a noncommuting role. This is not an attack on the earlier constitutive primitive role and candidatewise endpoint proof; it identifies the correct quantifier and temporal order.

52.3 A5 projective rank-two calculation independently reproduced

The direct controlling imported source is Paper I-A Paper1A-Geometry-v5_162.tex, theorems p1:thm:v340-cyclic-coset-design, p1:thm:v340-incidence-character-projectors, p1:thm:v340-reye-70-80, p1:thm:v341-coset-gram-hodge, and p1:thm:v341-desargues-rp2-parity. Paper II-F Paper2F-TechnicalMaterial-v1_57.tex, p2f:prop:v340-projective-binary-orthogonality and p2f:rem:v340-ranktwo-proof-scope, supplies the projective/binary bridge and explicit physical caution. These are inspected as direct imports, not globally reaudited peer papers.

The verifier independently constructs all 60 even permutations of five letters as A5=2I/{±1}, all element orders, all cyclic subgroups of orders 2,3,5 and all their left cosets. It gives:

Cyclic order Number of subgroups Projective cosets Binary great polygons
2 15 450 squares
3 10 200 hexagons
5 6 72 decagons
Total 31 722

Every unordered pair of the 60 projective points belongs to exactly one of these cosets; integer incidence matrices satisfy M₂+M₃+M₅=30I₆₀+J₆₀ in all 3,600 positions, and the pair count is 450+200·3+72·10=1770=C(60,2). Exact integer squared identities hold for N₅=M₂−M₃ and N₄=M₃−M₅:

N₅²=12N₅, N₄²=15N₄, N₅N₄=0, Tr(N₅)=300, Tr(N₄)=240.

Therefore P₅=N₅/12 and P₄=N₄/15 are orthogonal projectors of rank 25 and rank 16. The rank-25 image is not the 25-element transporter-label span: the 60×25 projective transporter matrix B_tr has rank 17 and N₅ B_tr=0. In the original 120-point binary lift the normalized centre-even pullback doubles the unnormalized Gram by a factor of two; no mixing of projective/binary counting conventions is justified.

For the five point-stabilizer A4 cores, their union is the 36-element set of identity, involutions and 3-cycles. Exactly 70 order-three cosets lie in at least one core, while 80 have all three vertices in the union. The difference is the ten (2,2,2) mixed-core hexagons. The 70-plane incidence matrix has exact integral left nullvector z=-2 on order-1/2 points and z=1 on order-3 points. Independent elimination modulo 1009 gives rank at least 35; the nullvector gives rational rank at most 35. Hence rank_Q R70=35. The 80-plane incidence matrix has mod-1009 rank 36, hence rank_Q R80=36 (all rows), exactly confirming the source's missing-ten-plane rank increase.

52.4 Signed Hodge and Desargues parity: mathematical marks versus physical effects

On the ten unordered pairs of five labels, the disjoint-pair adjacency A is Petersen. Build the signed Hodge S_H using orientation of the unique complementary fifth index for each disjoint pair pair. Integer checks prove S_H³=5 S_H. The source's second-neighbour operator T=S_H²−3I satisfies T(S_H)=T(−S_H) identically, so it loses the sign needed to separate the two signed-parity channels. This is an exact nonidentifiability observation, not an instruction to add a new parity postulate.

The Desargues bipartite cover has block adjacency [[0,A],[A,0]] and spectrum 3^(1),2^(4),1^(5),(-1)^(5),(-2)^(4),(-3)^(1). For all 120 permutations of five labels, the induced ordinary permutation action preserves A; the oriented exterior-square action sends S_H to sgn(π)S_H. In particular an odd permutation changes the signing while exchanging two RP² face sets. This is transport between marked mathematical presentations, not a symmetry of one frozen signed physical owner without the requisite action/effect certificate.

A proposed physical outcome would need (i) an occurring geometric packet, (ii) an independently licensed same-owner preparation/effect and its embedding, (iii) normalized measurable probabilities/poles, and (iv) a quantitative difference between actual allowed competitor supports/signings. None follows from the rank numerology or graph spectrum. The rank-15 finite coset projector also remains distinct from the intrinsic complex H₁ dimension 15; none of this changes m_p=1.

52.5 Older frontier remarks: scalar chronology, quartic isolation and conservative graphs

  • p2a:rem:v280-readout-graph-not-order-isomorphism (5136–5153). The two raw graph pairs (a,{0}) and (b,N) project bijectively onto {a,b}, yet GP1 rejects the complete unbounded generated output of b when that output is consumed. Thus the GP1-admissible structured graph is not a mere copy of the raw parent candidate preorder. An order isomorphism needs both conservative admissibility and preservation/reflection of whole-candidate GP3 comparisons; the separately source-repaired theorem already imposes those guards. The example does not assert physical realization of either toy candidate.
  • p2a:rem:v257-critical-quartic-control (5455–5468). Eliminate e from S(n,e)=e²/2+g e n²+(λ/4)n⁴ using e=−gn². The resulting (λ−2g²)n⁴/4 proves an isolated critical point when λ≠2g², a strict local minimum when positive, and a quartic decreasing direction when negative. When λ=2g², this exact toy polynomial has a stationary curve; a generic full action with zero quartic requires higher-order analysis. No unlicensed physical mass/zero mode is eliminated.
  • p2:rem:legacy-module-capacity-pointer (8675–8684). Optional truncated word-capacity formulas under separately registered P_sli / ceiling cannot be retrofitted as m_p=1 proof premises. The intrinsic geometry level-one identity and later generated momentum result remain distinct.
  • p2:rem:v193-u1-su2-provenance-firewall (8733–8741). Homomorphisms U(1)→SU(2) are mathematical cocharacters. They do not make a physical Hopf/succession bridge appear. The previously constituted HS_t arrow κ_t and chosen Hopf occurrence remain necessary when claimed.
  • p2a:rem:v3102-no-new-gp (10897–10902), p2a:rem:v3103-no-new-principle (10940–10945). Complete preterminal cross-return then scalarization is the earlier GP2-generated co-consumption logic. Later consuming both scalar m_r and relation ρ_r requires retaining the full paired tuple in the previously licensed word. Dropping ρ_r and postselecting it from m_r is not a generated relation; neither remark creates a mass/source target or a new constitutional principle. An exact two-channel unit-norm example u=(1,0), v=(0,1), w=(1,0) has identical individual squared norms but distinct mixed returns <u,v>=0, <u,w>=1, showing why the scalar alone cannot determine the cross relation.

53.1 Five newly reviewed source objects

Source object / location in v3.211 Verdict Exact scope and proof obligation
p2a:def:host-constructor-incidence-contract ~2298–2321 VERIFIED-TYPED-DEFINITION X≼_H H means constituted geometric support/hosting, not literal subset inclusion. A certified constructor arrow includes complete input, marks, codomain and transport of its retained incidence; a minimal host is minimal for the consumed word and support order, not all possible words.
p2a:cor:recursive-host-coverage ~2322–2338 VERIFIED-CONDITIONAL Finite induction. Base inputs hosted; at each step host-compatible licensed constructor consumes hosted inputs, produces a hosted output and transports exactly declared incidences. No inference that two outputs hosted by H are equal, or that every member of a role occurs.
p2a:thm:evaluation-dual-corealization ~2494–2568 VERIFIED-CONDITIONAL For total ε:X×E→S, evaluation-separating j:E↪S^X, generated T:X→X, and a previously frozen physical precomposition constructor with E closed under it, T^∨=j^{-1}P_Tj exists uniquely. The exact identity ε(Tx,e)=ε(x,T^∨e) yields the identity and contravariant composition law (T∘U)^∨=U^∨∘T^∨. The complete tuple and declared operation graphs are retained on a non-FRC-obstructed rooted branch, not a second G0 process. No negative physical time, CP map, Hamiltonian, calibration, or history follows.
p2a:cor:g0-witness-transport-multiroot-boundary ~2613–2622 VERIFIED-CONDITIONAL The origin certificate applies to the whole jointly occurring/generated input tuple. Removing a consumed calibration, source, provenance arrow or unresolved-member input invalidates the implication. Generating a history doctrine does not instantiate a particular history.
p2a:rem:g0-witness-transport-firewall ~2624–2652 VERIFIED-RELATIVE-NECESSITY Intrinsicness/naturality/faithfulness alone cannot add a physical constructor to a frozen grammar; counter-presentations with the same mathematical host but different licensed grammar separate the cases. The no-missing-input, declared-output/native-sort, and pre-output-frozen-word clauses are individually needed to exclude their stated kinds of illicit promotion. This is a premise-minimality analysis relative to the specified grammar, not uniqueness of its syntactic encoding.

Independent mathematical rederivation

Define the injective evaluation representation j(e)(x)=ε(x,e). A putative dual T^∨ must satisfy j(T^∨e)=P_T(j(e)), where P_T f=f∘T. Thus P_T(j(E))⊆j(E) is necessary and sufficient. Under this condition, j^{-1} is well-defined only on j(E) and gives the unique dual. Since P_{T∘U}=P_U P_T, the family of stabilizing maps is a monoid and dualization reverses composition. This mathematical existence proof does not supply the frozen physical precomposition license, typed target or co-realized root. Those enter independently through the stated origin certificate and the rooted GP2 theorem. The distinction prevents the conventional mathematical dual from being silently declared a new physical process.

For host coverage, induct on the finite length of the word. The result is an implication under host compatibility of every required arrow, not an unconditional theorem for arbitrary set-theoretic functions. Two distinct functions on {0,1}, f(x)=x and g(x)=1−x, may both have outputs hosted in one H={0,1}; common hosting does not entail same output. For nonseparating evaluation, X={*}, E={a,b} and ε(*,a)=ε(*,b)=0, even the identity on X admits four possible maps E→E preserving all evaluations; evaluation separation is indispensable for uniqueness. With X={0,1}, test family E={x↦x} and T(x)=1−x, f∘T∉E, so no dual can be generated without an enlarged test family/typed constructor.

53.2 GP3 principle and repeated-label reconciliation

The p2:post:GP3 source ~3732–3814 is foundational postulate content, already examined in prior batches, not a new independently proved theorem. It defines complete structured GP1/GP2 candidate comparison, distinguishes least from minimal, and prevents a raw carrier-size/operation-count selector. In a nonempty preorder, a least class, if established, is irreducible; where a distinct survivor family is claimed, its nonemptiness must also be justified. In a general infinite preorder, existence of a minimal/irreducible element does not follow from GP1 finiteness of each candidate. Example: candidates indexed by positive integers, with M_j≺M_i for j>i, have no irreducible member. This is a scope challenge, not a new in-domain refutation of the primitive endpoints: their already audited candidatewise Lie-image/exactification route establishes an actual least skeleton under the stated role hypotheses. No new issue is certified without an actual consumer relying on nonexistent minimal members; the retained common-readout theorems already require a nonempty surviving family.

Four missing ledger labels were aliases of earlier audited theorems: p2a:thm:g0-witness-transport-derived-constructor is the same environment as p2a:thm:rooted-generated-image-corealization; p2:thm:gp-least-role-chain and p2:thm:gp-minimal-role-chain are the same master allocation theorem as p2:thm:master-compact-role-allocation; p2:thm:gp2-generated-rank-one-obstruction is the same rank-one theorem as p2:thm:gp2-commutator-dichotomy. They must not inflate the number of independent source proof objects. The nested p2:sr:no-extra-marked-datum is an explicit clause of the already audited definition p2:def:structured-role-realization (~882–939), not a freestanding theorem. The equation and navigation anchors are source locators; their mathematical equations remain part of their containing claims.

53.3 Repair of BH-IIA-CONV-ID-001 (previously OPEN)

Actual source mismatch in v3.210, p2a:thm:v274-two-scalar-repeatability-certificate lines 9578–9580: its two displayed valid mathematical moment tests were called equivalent to repeatability of the same two polynomial functions under the left averaging action at the identity, even though A_μ f(x)=∫f(g^{-1}x)dμ(g). The correct identity is

A_μ²(f∘inv)(e) = ∫ f d(μ*μ), A_μ(f∘inv)(e) = ∫ f dμ.

Source repair v3.211: qualify the identity-point tests as p_S∘inv and h_μ∘inv, give both displayed identities, and explicitly reject the old claim for the unmodified functions. Quaternion inversion is an orthogonal linear map on the ambient R⁴, so the respective degrees N and ≤N−1 are unchanged. Both moment conditions and their quantitative bounds remain unchanged. No independent function-valued test becomes physically available without a licensed same-owner test/averaging role. The B49 exact C5 counterexample to the old gloss is retained as an adversarial regression: μ=δ_a, a^5=e, a≠e, with polynomial p_S(q)=1−⟨q,a⟩, has A_μ p_S(e)=A_μ²p_S(e)=(5+√5)/4, although ∫p_S d(μ*μ)=(5−√5)/4>0 and {a} is not a group. The revised inverse-pulled test correctly fails. The B50 finite-band theorem's inverse-pulled proof remains an independent intact bypass.

Issue disposition: BH-IIA-CONV-ID-001 is now RESOLVED IN SOURCE (v3.211), not a theorem saying every finitely supported convolution operation physically occurs. The old incorrect historical route remains archived as a blocked v3.210 route, while a new corrected v3.211 identity route is verified. The other eight prior issue records remain resolved within their existing scopes. Zero tracked paper issues remain open after this source repair; potential gaps in unreviewed steps or other papers are not thereby ruled out.

Born–Hopf Geometry — Paper II-A proof-chain completeness audit, Batch 54

Date: 9 November 2026. Scope: indispensable unlabeled implications and direct Paper I-A / II-B / II-C / II-F imports, not another census of Paper II-A theorem labels. Frozen input: Framework v2.363, Paper II-A v3.211, Paper II-B v2.135, Paper II-C v2.160, Paper II-F v1.57. Updated source: Paper II-A v3.212; Paper II-F v1.58; Framework v2.364. Audit output: this certificate, cumulative Paper2A-ProofChainAudit-v1_54.md, successor DAG Atlas v1.69.

1. Verdict and identified earliest material boundary

The upstream Paper II-B theorem p2:thm:momentum-host-H1 (source 1112–1151) proves the complete generated six-type support for the frozen momentum seed. Its separate Corollary p2:thm:m1-component (1367–1384) proves its intrinsic half-word level m_p=1 for every GP1-admissible, GP2-exact realization of that seed even with arbitrary positive implementation multiplicities. This is a genuine unconditional level statement relative to the frozen primitive seed. The canonical multiplicity-free model has dimension 1+2+2+4+3+3=15.

But Paper II-B's p2b:thm:momentum-carrier-rigidity (1436–1484) explicitly states that the stronger quantified conclusion every GP3-irreducible full structured carrier H_p ≅ H_1 requires three independent, whole-structured comparison certificates, not just the common six-type support. That paper identifies the universal candidate-independent comparison as still an outstanding obligation. The directly inspected Paper II-C (287–333) and III-A (510–529 and 1148–1165) already maintain this distinction. Prior Paper II-A overview statements (v3.211, 281–288; 594–607; 8945 ff.; conclusion around 11920) and Paper II-F v1.57 (3098–3104; 3138–3144; 3711–3717; theorem 3733–3749; 4177–4185; 4199–4205) used the strong wording without carrying the conditional GP3 comparison premise.

Certified source-local issue BH-IIA-H1-IMPORT-001: RESOLVED IN SOURCE, not by imposing a new physical premise, but by distinguishing the propositions exactly at their proved strengths and updating both consuming manuscripts. The corrected chronology is

[ \operatorname{Gen}_{\Sigma_p}^{\mathrm{car}}\cong\mathcal H_1 \quad\Longrightarrow\quad m_p=1, ]

with the separate guarded implication

[ [\mathrm{CMP}(i)\land\mathrm{CMP}(ii)\land\mathrm{CMP}(iii)] \quad\Longrightarrow\quad \bigl(\text{every GP3-irreducible full endpoint }\mathcal H_p\cong\mathcal H_1\bigr). ]

Important: The second implication is not thereby unconditionally discharged. BH-IIB-STRUCTURED-RIGIDITY-001 is the already explicit, still-open upstream whole-structured comparison proof obligation. It does not block the round parent, the six-type support, m_p=1, its canonical 15-dimensional model, or calculations stated on that model. No actual multiplicity-rich GP2-exact counterexample has been supplied, and no new GP4 is introduced.

3. Independent derivations and adversarial controls

A. Bracket image and the generated six types

For S³(R) and f_u(x)=u·x, the exact Riemannian gradient is G_u=u−(u·x)x/R². Differentiating in the ambient coordinates and antisymmetrizing on the sphere yields

[ [G_u,G_v]=R^{-2}\bigl((u\cdot x)v-(v\cdot x)u\bigr). ]

Hence the linear span, not the literal two-variable image, is canonically Λ²R⁴ ≅ so(4) (real dimension 6), which splits after complexification as (1,0)⊕(0,1). Its co-generated first scalar, spinor and gradient modules yield H_1=1⊕S_+⊕S_-⊕E_1⊕K_{Kill,C} with dimension 15. Whether those object and operation sorts are physically retained is governed by the frozen II-A/II-B constructor grammar, not this differential identity alone.

B. Minimal level does not prove universal one-copy realization

If the irreducible types are T_p, a general finite representation with this exact type-support can have multiplicities m_λ≥1 and

[ \dim\mathcal H-15=\sum_{\lambda\in T_p}d_\lambda(m_\lambda-1),\qquad \dim\operatorname{End}{\mathrm{Spin}(4)}\mathcal H-6 =\sum{\lambda\in T_p}(m_\lambda^2-1). ]

For example, H_1⊕(0,0) still has six type classes, m_p=1, and full dimension 16 (rather than 15); its equivariant commutant dimension is 9 (rather than 6). The character-squared diagnostic equals Σ(m_λ−1)². These are mathematical counterexamples to the inference “same support ⇒ one copy.” They are not certified physical candidates: a GP2 exactness test and the frozen role's complete test/operation diagrams would still have to be checked. Their utility is to demonstrate precisely why Paper II-B has three stronger comparison hypotheses. The framework's observable question is: Which licensed same-owner tests or constructors can discriminate the multiplicity sectors, and is the universal generated comparison preserving all of them? Absent such evidence, neither a new physical multiplicity nor universal absence of it is selected.

C. Inverse/dual and independent joint support

On a concrete unit-quaternion subgroup Q₈={±1,±i,±j,±k}, let first-harmonic evaluation represent a by its four coordinate values. This separates points. For any g, the induced evaluation-dual action is g∨(a)=g^{-1}a, and ((gh)∨)(a)=h∨(g∨(a)). The possible joint pairs are 8×8=64, whereas a graph of one chosen role isomorphism has only eight points. This is a finite algebraic check of the independence-versus-graph logical distinction, not a derivation of physical product occurrence from Q₈ or of arbitrary marked 2I support.

D. Exact reproducibility

Paper2A-CrossPaperProofBatch54-v1_1-verify.py reports 23,916/23,916 PASS deterministic rational/integer assertions: 512 quaternion associativity, 64 closure and inverse cases, 512 dual anti-composition and 512 norm checks, 64 point-separation and 64 total pairings, 810 exact gradient-bracket calculations and 810 tangency checks, and 4,096 multiplicity vectors each checked for type support, dimension excess, equivariant commutant, endpoint one-copy criterion and character defect, plus source/import/label guards. The tests confirm only these finite mathematical facts and source-check contracts; they do not verify arbitrary infinite GP3 candidate comparisons or the occurrence of a physical detector.
